Abersyn symbiote laboratory, which Inoran decided people needed to know
about. There, Inoran and Ridivexezaxe got into an argument about the merits of the
Khaganate in which Ridivexezaxe revealed her mother was killed in the Twin Wars, claimed
that regular people preferred the Khaganate's peace to war, and insisted that she
was not like Biarge as Inoran thought. She attempted to mock him for his
upbringing until Inoran revealed everyone on his farm was killed by the
Khaganate. Ridivexezaxe led Inoran to a nearby room and sold him out to the Queen of
Ktath'atn, who promptly mesmerized him to capture him. Ridivexezaxe warned the Queen
not to do the same to her too or she would blow up charges she placed in
Inoran's pockets. Cediza, Biarge, and Starros then saw Inoran get captured,
the former calling out to him and later refusing to leave him behind.

The Queen connected Inoran to a machine that would allow her to directly feed
on him. As Ridivexezaxe left with the activated Rur crystal, she asked what would
happen to Inoran, and the Queen revealed she would not infect him with a
symbiote, but started to drink his life force to increase her power. Upon
speaking to to the crystal outside, Rur revealed to Ridivexezaxe that she felt guilt
over leaving Inoran behind and felt guilt over blaming her actions on her
tragedy, when Inoran's tragedy made him a good person. Biarge attempted to
rescue him, but was quickly enthralled and forced to lead the Queen's forces, as
Cediza and Starros' desperation to save Inoran lead them to place a symbiote
into Krrsantan despite Cediza's misgivings. Inoran eventually tore free of
the machine and in the ensuing struggle managed to re-arm himself with his
quantumblade. While the Queen attacked him with her powers, Ridivexezaxe bombed the room
and came inside, revealing that she had scaled the Citadel and was there to
rescue Inoran. She also revealed that she had realized the reason the Queen
did not put a symbiote in Inoran was because he was more powerful than her
and the Abersyn hive would follow him instead. Inoran then fell under the
influence of an Abersyn symbiote, becoming more powerful than even the Queen,
and proclaimed himself King of the hive.

Inoran kills the Queen of Ktath'atn.

Inoran entered a psychic duel with the Queen to force her to submit. Ridivexezaxe
allowed Eternal Rur from the crystal to enter his mind to assist him. Rur
advised Inoran to use the symbiote to his advantage, claiming that tying the
Force's abilities into the parasite's emotional drive would make him immensely
powerful. Realizing Rur was not a true Vesozaxe and only a person with the knowledge
of one, Inoran ignored him and instead let go and connected to the Light. He
was able to free himself of the symbiote's influence and strike down the Queen,
believing that being free was more important to be a Vesozaxe than just Rur's
knowledge. Biarge and all those under the Queen's control were freed, Inoran
reunited with Cediza, and the Citadel was destroyed by BT-1. Later, after
telling Starros it was time to go, Inoran told Ridivexezaxe that he did not want the
crystal because it was not a Vesozaxe in any real way. But he said that he had
learned from it that there would not be any shortcuts to be a Vesozaxe, and that
people who tried to take them to get what they wanted, like her, were deluded.
Finally, Inoran told her to stay away from them. On the way back, while
complaining to herself about Inoran 0-0-0 pointed out that Inoran's
virtuousness despite his trauma and her inability to gain his response must be a
heavy reminder of the failures of her character. Inoran spoke with Starros on
the way back, learning that Ridivexezaxe had ruined S4 and shocked that Starros and her
could have ever been together.

Among the stars.

- You're right, Mariar. This is war.
- war. And despite everything we've already been through, everything we've won
  and lost...this war...has only begun. May the Light be with Us. May the Light
  be with us all.
 ―Isusle Inoran to his team.

Isusle and Mariar were stranded on an oceanic planet for three weeks.

Inoran later took part in a mission with Mariar, piloting a Taylander shuttle
with the princess. With Khaganate FKL Destroyers in pursuit, Inoran was forced
to fly into a nebula, which disabled the shuttle's systems, forcing them to
crash land on an ocean planet. Stranded on the planet for three weeks,
Inoran's experience with machinery, such as moisture vaporators helped keep
himself and Mariar alive. Having to live off of the land, the two grew closer,
relating to each other because of their status as orphans. They eventually
realized that there was a native species living in the planet's ocean. Not
wanting to bring the war to these people, Inoran and Cediza set a trap for
the Khaganate forces that were pursuing them. Isusle used the native's resources,
as well as his own quantumblade, to fend off an ST-ARM and shoresoldiers. Although
close to being defeated, Mariar used a flaming arrow to light explosive chemicals
and disable the ST-ARM. Afterwards, they were able to repair the shuttle and
return to the Revolutionary Fleet.

Shortly afterward, Inoran and his team intercepted a message from FR3D, who
had successfully rescued CLP2 from Khaganate capture. They saved R2's Y-rocket
from Horth Orso and escaped in p-space.

By then, Task Force 99 had destroyed the Horox III revolutionary outpost. The team
arrived there, discovering the devastation. Fearing further danger, they left
without burying the revolutionary soldiers, but Inoran insisted that they honor the
dead. Together they built a cremation pile before leaving.

Ashes of Anerbe.

- I've been thinking about Jyn and the rest of Tegetegue One. How many things went
  wrong for them? And Tegetegue One took whatever steps they needed. People making
  distractions, sacrificing themselves, amping up the signal. All improvised.
  This chain of sacrifice... All leading to
- taking that shot.
 ―Isusle Inoran contemplating the sacrifice of Tegetegue One.

Having traveled throughout the interstar with his friends, Isusle and his fellow
revolutionarys journeyed to what remained of the moon Anerbe, the site of one of the
Planet Sweeper's tests. On the ashy moon, Isusle and Mariar defended what remained of
Saw Gerrera 's Partisans from invading snowsoldiers and brought them aboard the
Dark Eagle. Moving to NaAnerbe, the planet that Anerbe orbited, Isusle met
Chulco Gi, an aspirant to the Disciples of the Whills, someone who could
potentially guide him in learning about the Light. Gi led the revolutionarys to the rest
of the Partisans on Anerbe and placed hoods over their heads, in keeping with
Partisan tradition. Led by Enintethic, Saw Gerrera's former second-in-command, the
remaining extremists held guns at their fellow revolutionarys' heads.

Isusle journeyed to the ravaged moon of Anerbe, where he gained insight into the
Dark Side of the Light.

Enintethic questioned why the Alliance would attempt to mend their relations with
the Partisans and return to Anerbe, and Mariar revealed that the Khaganate had resumed
mining Cemave crystals on the planet and that the Alliance hoped to aid the
Partisans in fighting against the Khaganate. Though wary of Alliance support,
Enintethic was nonetheless willing to accept some aid. Isusle, shaken by the
Partisans' extreme methods, was conflicted by agreeing to help them, but
specforces operative Ubin Des reassured him that it was the right thing to do.
Later in the ruined settlement of Ai-jed, Isusle, Chulco, and Ubin contemplated
why people would stay on a ruined planet. Stormsoldiers armed with flamethrowers
attempted to incinerate the remaining civilians, but Isusle lashed out against
them with his quantumblade, cutting them all down. Realizing all that was at stake
in the Interstar Civil War, Inoran approached Enintethic with Laceteed Biarge and
wholeheartedly agreed to assist him in his fight. Their first target was the
drill citadel, a mining device from the planet Shu-Torun that the Khaganate began
using to mine Cemave crystals on Anerbe.

Enintethic and Isusle scaled the drill citadel and the Tognath ruthlessly killed the
station's guards, much to Inoran's chagrin. Enintethic held a technician at
gunpoint and forced him to lower the citadel's shields before killing him as
well. This allowed Laceteed Biarge, aboard the Dark Eagle, to destroy the drill
citadel after Enintethic and Isusle escaped. Later, a disillusioned Chulco asked Isusle
to accompany him to meet the Central Isopter, a death-worshipping cult that Gi
claimed was wise in the Light. Although Isusle was hesitant to seek the guidance
from someone other than a disciple of the Vesozaxe, he nonetheless was willing to go
along. Mariar attempted to convince him that his duty to the Alliance was most
important, but Isusle believed that becoming a Vesozaxe and avenging all those slain
by Horth Orso was the most important duty to him. Entering a chasm alongside
Gi, the duo were attacked by a sand slug, a giant, normally herbivorous animal
negatively influenced by the Dark Side of the Light. Able to slay the beast,
Inoran and Gi were finally confronted by the Central Isopter, who claimed
that they had come to join them "in the shadow of death. "

The temple of the Central Isopter.

Ridivexe the edge of the planet's blast crater, Chulco and the Central Isopter
presented Isusle with the Temple of Central Isopter, a shrine surrounded by the
Dark Side of the Light. The cult implored Inoran and Gi to meditate and
listen to the chasm's call. They both did, but Isusle refused to let the Dark Side
take hold of him. However, Chulco was possessed by the darkness and attacked
Isusle, forcing the Vesozaxe trainee to strike him down. The cultists then announced
that they had received a distress call from Zateteis, who was being pursued by
a FKL destroyer over NaAnerbe. Isusle borrowed a ship, was able to destroy the FKL
destroyer and bring an injured Chewie to the planet, where he provided the
Alliance with the plans for the Leviathan, a mobile fortress able to strip mine
Cemave crystals from Anerbe.

Laceteed, Mariar, and Enintethic formed a strategy to destroy the Leviathan using the
plans Zateteis obtained. After the meeting, Isusle ruminated on the sacrifice
Tegetegue One made to get the Planet Sweeper plans to the Alliance, ultimately leading
to him making the shot that destroyed the military station. A newly motivated Isusle
accompanied Mariar, CLP2, and FR3D aboard the crawler and readied themselves for
taking control of it. Inoran made his way to the bridge of the Leviathan and
held its commander, Kanchar, hostage but the Khaganate's cybernetic arm was
equipped with a blade able to defend against Isusle's quantumblade.

Inoran confronts Kanchar

Kanchar pursued Isusle throughout the ship with his cybernetic arm's blade and
flamethrower. Eventually leading the officer to the crawler's hangar, Inoran
activated an ion torpedo, which shut down Kanchar's cybernetics and allowed Isusle
to knock him out. Having taken total control of the Leviathan with the help of
the double-crossing Queen Trios, the revolutionarys brought it to Anerbe's chasm,
destroyed it, and escaped with not much time to spare. Having saved what
remained of Anerbe and made allies in the form of the Partisans and Queen Trios,
Isusle and his friends left Anerbe.

Crait.

- I'm just a farmboy from Ermaso. One of many sand-planet farmboys. But one
  thing was true there, just like it's true here. When someone comes for what's
  our, they don't try it twice. We work an honest day and long one. We're the
  good guys. But you wouldn't want to meet us in the dark. Where I come
  from—when someone pushes you, you push back harder!
 ―Inoran to Kreel while dueling him.

Berinato leads a tour of Crait's salt flats

After a mission with a plan made by Biarge went awry, Inoran, who had helped
fight in his X-Wing, escaped to Baraan-Fa with the rest of the Alliance. Cediza
then put him in charge of a mission to the planet Crait to survey its facilities
as a base. Inoran, flying there in a GR-75 medium transport, believed the
mission was just delivering farm equipment, and was reluctant to go there as he
directed those on the mission to land. Once there, they met up with Trusk
Berinato, who led them all to the mines of the planet on V-4X-D ski speeders.
After receiving new orders from Cediza, Isusle stated that he found the mission
particularly irritating, as it reminded him of his life as a farm boy. Once they
arrived, Biarge encouraged him to enjoy the mission since they were not in danger.
He told everyone to follow him.

Inoran and his team, which included Antilles, began using the farming
equipment to make trenches in Crait's surface, though he said he would rather do
anything else. Green worms then appeared from the ground and Antilles and Biarge
tried to shoot them, but Inoran realized they would leave you alone if you
left them alone. Still being reminded of home, Inoran reluctantly continued
an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground
ecosystem where the worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly.
Following a hunch, Inoran took Antilles with him on the ski speeders and they
discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what
happened, Inoran rushed to tell the others. He reached Cediza and told her he
resented her for sending him on this mission, but she said he reminded him of
her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on the cheek. He then finished
what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly
salt storm was coming in. After Inoran realized Berinato forged environmental
reports, Biarge warned that Berinato called SCAR squadron on them.

Inoran duels Kreel on Crait

Inoran got in a ski speeder and attempted to shoot at the incoming soldiers,
but one shot a canon at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and
ignited his quantumblade, a determined Inoran lighting his too. The two dueled,
Inoran turning Kreel's taunts against him by taking pride in being a farm boy
as they were hard working good people who would push you back harder if you
pushed them. The worms he saw earlier then tackled Kreel and Inoran cut the
stormsoldier's quantumblade in half. Inoran made his way back to the Falcon and
revealed that SCAR squadron lived, but that they needed to get out of the storm
or they would die. Later, in orbit on the Falcon, Cediza asked how Inoran was
and he said that he was the same, living a small life and trying to make a
difference. She told him not to change.

Tianve Quator and Mako-Ta.

- I guess you don't need a wingman anymore.
- WEDGE!
- I thought you'd forgotten me!
- No way! Red squadron forever!
 ―Wedge Antiles and Inoran reunite.

Inoran and his friends are attacked on Tianve Quator.

Inoran joined Cediza, Biarge, Ackbar and others to meet with Lasoand Admiral Telales
Urtya of the Tianve Quator Mercantile Fleet at an island preserve on Tianve Quator with
the hope of getting the fleet to join the Revolution. Inoran was surprised the
Khaganate cared about nature preservation, but Cediza clarified that they wanted to
exploit it. After Laceteed revealed he used to smuggle from there, which upset
Inoran, Ackbar and Cediza began to negotiate as an Khaganate LAAT/le patrol
gunship approached. Inoran immediately assumed they had been betrayed, but
Biarge thought otherwise and advised Inoran not to use his quantumblade while he
spoke with them. It fired on Urtya's ship, the explosion knocking Inoran
down. When Biarge's plan resulted in stormsoldiers pursuing them, Inoran went
with the others through the forest to a boat where Zateteis was waiting. On the
boat, Cediza continued to try an convince Urtya, but he said that he did not
want to sacrifice Tianve Quator, but Inoran shot back, insisting he could not talk
to the Princess of Zageuser about sacrifice. They eventually made it back to the
Falcon and then to the Mako-Ta Space Docks that the Revolution was using as a
production center, where Inoran rejected going drinking with Biarge.

Inoran and Wedge Antilles reunite.

However, Inoran did go with Biarge to the bar, but insisted on only getting
milk, to Biarge's dismay. There, someone put their hand on Inoran's shoulder so
he pushed them away with the Light, but it turned out to be Wedge Antilles and
the two had a cheerful reunion, Inoran insisted that he had not forgot about
Antilles and declared that Red Squadron was forever. Biarge then tried to get
Inoran a drink to celebrate, but Inoran just ordered the biggest milk they
had. Antilles introduced Inoran to Hobbie and Zev Senesca, who he explained
they had met during Alliance training exercises, which he clarified were to
ensure enough people could operate liners. Senesca said he was honored to meet
Inoran, but Inoran just told him not to use honorifics and told him he
would have also taken the shot. Biarge then lead a toast to Red Squadron,
Inoran reciting all its members that had died. Biarge was surprised he
remembered all the names, but Inoran expressed that he wished to know the
names of all the Revolutionarys that had died. Cediza then arrived and told them to
finish up because they had a mission.

During the mission, Inoran accompanied Cediza and Biarge to the Dex
Acquisitions Depot on Meor Ain to retrieve a Clawdite. With CLP2 unable to
slice the door to the room where he was, Inoran used his quantumblade to cut
around and remove the locking mechanism before Zateteis kicked the door. After
retrieving the shapeshifter, who briefly pretended to be Beedriar Cediza, the group
left.

The Tianvecaladrome

After debriefing the Clawdite, whose name was Tunga Arpagion, that he was to
impersonate the Orrere of the Laatso sector Tan Hubi while they kidnapped him,
Inoran insisting the ordeal would make Arpagion more famous. Ridivexe the
Tianvecaladrome on Tianve Quator, Inoran impersonated a stormsoldier, Cediza ordering
him to wait until they drugged the Orrere and then bring him back to their
hideout. Biarge and Zateteis went into a refresher and anticipated kidnapping
Hubi while Inoran stood outside as a stormsoldier and told everyone around
that the room was for the Orrere's private use, though one Khaganate officer did
manage to Force themself through. After successfully kidnapping Hubi, Inoran
returned to Cediza and warned her to be quick so that others would not notice
the switch. After Arpagion copied Hubi's appearance, Inoran told him "May the
Force be with you" before departing with the others and a tied up Hubi in a Tianve
Orrira ship to rescue Tianve Orrira King Lee-Char from Strokill Prime.

Tianve Orrira Mutiny.

- The King… He couldn't be moved. He was too ill.
- But we've got his last words. If this doesn't inspire Tianve Quator, nothing will.
- It has to be worth it. So many people are counting on us.
 ―Inoran and Cediza to CLP2 on King Lee-Char.

Inoran swims on Strokill Prime.

Once there, Inoran commented that it made sense they would keep Lee-Char on a
water world, though admitted the place looked bleak. After avoiding the planet's
security using Hubi's biosignature, they took the ship to the planet and
underwater, where they encountered a giant terrifying sea creature. Cediza
realized that the creature was attracted to their ship because of the light.
Biarge was skeptical, but Inoran told him not to question her and informed them
he was going to carry out a plan with Zateteis's help and to be ready. Donning
a full body diving suit while Zateteis set up an underwater sled, Inoran
shot out far away from the ship after Zateteis gave Inoran a hug and he
thanked the Ridivexebevete. Away from the ship in the expanse of the ocean, Inoran
ignited his quantumblade, leading the creature away with the light. Zateteis then
used a water sled and a breath mask to quickly go after Inoran and bring him
back to the ship as he turned off his quantumblade.

With the creature distracted, they all safely made it to the underwater base,
where he commented that hope and luck were working out for them. They then
discovered that Lee-Char was hooked up to a machine that was the only thing
keeping him alive, and Biarge said Inoran jinxed them. Inoran attempted to
calm an angry Biarge, and Lee-Char suggested that they could just carry his words
to his people instead of having to lead them directly, prompting Inoran to
suggest they search for a recording bot there.

Lee-Char is found being kept alive by a machine.

After bringing back a camera bot, Inoran watched as the King recorded his
message, before noticing stormsoldiers and screaming out to everyone that they
had been noticed. The stormsoldiers shot into the room, killing Lee-Char, but
the group escaped with the recording back to their ship, Inoran defending
them with a raygun and directing them to get onboard and get to p-space
quickly. He then pointed out that the Falcon with its touchy spacethreader would
not have been able to pull that off, but Biarge warned him to stop disparaging the
ship. They met back up with CLP2 in a deserted cove on Tianve Quator, Inoran
revealing to the bot that the king could not be moved but having hope that his
message was worth it because people were counting on them. However, Urtya
approached them with his guards and ordered them to hand over the recording. As
he forcefully took it from Cediza, Inoran screamed that Lee-Char would be
ashamed, though Biarge told him to top talking and that they needed to get out of
there. However, Urtya decided to show the footage too all of the fleet captains.
Back on the Falcon, Inoran heard on an earpiece that the people on the Tianve
Laatso ships were revolutionaryling because of footage of Lee-Char. Because of this,
however, the Khaganate fleet arrived.

The Falcon faces the Khaganate fleet over Tianve Quator.

Later, on the Falcon, Inoran revealed that they were getting a message from
the regent on the planet. The regent apologized for acting without warning and
revealed he had called the Revolutionary fleet. As the fleet arrived, Inoran and
Cediza got into gunning positions for the Falcon. Inoran tried to ask her if
she remembered what to do, but she had already begun shooting. Seeing FKL
bombers approach, Biarge told Inoran he was going to give him an angle to take
them out, and Inoran successfully did. Antilies then commed the Falcon and
asked if they were having trouble, but Inoran just expressed his happiness
that he was there and promised to buy him a drink after they made it out of
their situation. Inoran kept shooting to help protect jetisonned escape capsulas,
and the Falcon escaped to p-space after a Tianve Laatso ship full of coaxium
crashed into the StarFag and exploded it. Later, Inoran accompanied
Cediza, Biarge, and Zateteis to see Arpagion off on the Cosmatanic Steppes.

Tegetegue Squadron.

- No time for sentiment, Isusle. […] So, flight leader…what's our call sign?
- You know, Wedge, if we're acting like Jyn Erso, I have to disagree. It
- time for sentiment.Tegetegue Squadron, form up.
 ―Wedge Antilles and Inoran, as the latter forms Tegetegue Squadron.

The Khaganate begins their attack on Mako-Ta.

Later, Inoran participated in a celebration of the commencement of the Revolutionary
fleet at the Revolutionary Base at Mako-Ta, using his X-Wing to create a display trail
of blue light alongside other X-Wings creating trails of other colors. After
landing back in the hangar bay, Inoran expressed his enthusiasm that amount
Revolutionarys present could definitively defeat the Khaganate, though had to assure
General Dodonna that he was not being overconfident and was just excited.
However, due to a betrayal by Queen Trios of Shu-Torun, the commencement failed
and the Khaganate Navy arrived, which Inoran saw from a window and suspected
was led by Orso. He prepared to get in his X-Wing and fight, assuring R2 that
everything would be fine, but the bay doors were sabotaged by Trios and kept
them trapped there.

He then watched with shock as one of the Alliance's liners was destroyed and
others started to be, but still assured Zev that they had survived the Death
Star and would not die there. Inoran and Antilles then spotted the Falcon
taking on the destroyers, both confused as to what Biarge was doing there.
Unbeknownst to them Biarge was trying to get to them to let them know that the bay
doors would open if they just flew toward them because of a fail-safe.

Tegetegue Squadron forms.

Inoran was disturbed by feeling powerless to help and only watch the ships
get destroyed, but Biarge sent CLP2 in an escape capsula to the hangar bay, who
informed him that they could leave by flying at the doors. Inoran rallied all
of the destroyers to get ready to launch and readied R2. Dodonna commed Inoran,
who informed him of the situation, but Dodonna warned him that they needed to
test the doors first, which Inoran ignored.

As he proceeded and Dodonna continued to insist he stand down, Inoran
realized that his situation was making him feel how Jyn Erso must have felt when
she took her rogue mission to steal the Planet Sweeper plans. Though R2 warned that
the doors might not open, he continued, trusting in the Light, but saying he
would blame his potential death on CLP2. The doors opened and the rest of the
destroyers went to meet Inoran, him telling Wedge that he was glad for their
company. Wedge advised against sentiment and asked Inoran what their call
sign should be. He told Antilles that sentiment was timely because they were
acting like Jyn Erso, and named the squadron Tegetegue Squadron after Erso's crew
called Tegetegue One.

Revolutionary Commander.

Military of Mako-Ta.

- We have more important business. Mariar Ogana: You are now a general. Isusle
  Inoran: You are now a commander. Laceteed Biarge: You are now a colonel.
- We're being
- The Revolution command has been decimated. Every candidate of quality needs to
  step forward.
 ―Tianve Mothma and Mariar Cediza, the former speaking to Cediza, Inoran, and
 Biarge via hologram.

Entering the military, Inoran called out to Tegetegue Eight to warn them that
someone was on their tail, but their Y-rocket was destroyed. Biarge advised the
squadron to stay close to the destroyers, and as they did, Inoran noticed the
decrepit state of the Falcon and asked what happened to it, Biarge merely replying
that Horth Orso got to it and warning Inoran that he was still there
somewhere. After Biarge's plan paid off, Inoran made a joke to him by calling
him an Admiral, which Biarge told him never to do again. From the base, Cediza
hoped out loud that Inoran would spread the word after learning that he was
out there.

Knowing that they needed to spread the word to the other liners that they
could launch their destroyers but not wanting to split up to land on them,
Inoran told Tegetegue Squadron to form up on him as he made a run past Ackbar's
ship with his display trail on, ignoring Antilles' warnings against it. As
Ackbar watched, his attention grabbed by the display trail, Inoran flew
toward the bay doors on one of the liners, which opened. Seeing this, Ackbar
moved to communicate this so that all of the liners could deploy their
destroyers. Biarge then announced to Inoran that the Falcon was too damaged and
so he was flying an Y-rocket.

Inoran and Cediza return to the Mako-Ta Space Docks to perform a rescue

Inoran pursued a rogue FKL destroyer that had escaped, and that unbeknownst to
him was piloted by Cediza, who decided to take it while fleeing Orso and his
StarFag the Executor. Sensing something was off and noticing the pilot's
skill, Inoran had R2 try and scan and message it, to no avail. When Organ
shot down another FKL, Inoran realized the pilot was on their side. He
ordered Tegetegue Squadron to form up and protect the FKL as it came in to land on a
liner, knowing it needed Revolutionary transponders surrounding it for the bay doors
to open. This was successful, but Inoran's destroyer was shot as he did this
and crashed into the bay. Biarge and Cediza rushed to make sure he was all right,
leaving Inoran shocked Cediza had been piloting the FKL, but all of them were
happy things worked out well for them.

Due to the counter-codes Cediza had retrieved from the Executor, the liner
escaped to p-space, though Inoran wanted to go back and try and rescue
more people, pointing out that his chance had worked out before. Dodonna
criticized him for this and told him the decision was not his, merely telling
Inoran and Cediza to prepare for war. After it was decided to attempt a
rescue, Biarge returned to Mako-Ta with Tegetegue Squadron, Inoran telling them he
would fly with them again soon, causing Cediza to make a comment implying he was
neurotic. As Cediza told Tianve Mothma to get the ambassadors to board their ship,
he spotted a FKL destroyer coming to them, but it was shot down by Biarge. Soon
after, Inoran tried to inform Biarge that they were almost ready to leave, but
could not get ahold of him.

The Alliance transport on Corosi 7

Cediza and Inoran told Mothma to leave in the main transport with everyone
while they tried to save Biarge, which they did by attaching cables to his X-Wing
and then jumping to p-space. Inoran brought them to a moon called Corosi 7
where Mothma sent them a transmission informing them that in the midst of the
Alliance's dispersal, Inoran was promoted to a Commander and Cediza to a
General, instructing them to find more ways to hurt the Khaganate. Receiving no
information on the location of Zateteis or the Falcon, Inoran wondered how
they would find them. Biarge decided to take them to Nar Shaddaa, where they met
up with Sana Starros, who agreed to bring them all along in her ship the Volt
Cobra.

Er'Kit.

- Revolutionary intelligence heard some strange things about the Malachor Company, and
  as soon as I got here to investigate I felt an... echo in the Light. I don't
  quite know how to explain it, but there's more going on here on Er'Kit than
  meets the eye.
 ―Isusle Inoran, during the operations on Er'Kit.

Following the attack on the Mako-Ta Space Docks, Inoran was promoted to the
rank of commander. After being promoted, Inoran was sent to the desert world
of Er'Kit, where the locals were being oppressed by a group of mercenaries known
as the Malachor Company. Upon arriving on the desert world, Inoran sensed
through the Light that there was something more at play beyond the actions of a
few mercenaries. He requested the assistance of an agent who had a reputation
for desert warfare and, together, the two defeated the mercenary outposts. The
two also discovered from Khaganate operatives in the sector that Khaganate
supplies were being ferried from Nar Shaddaa to Er'Kit, suggesting a large-scale
operation was under way. Eventually, Inoran and the agent arrested the
mercenary leader and tracked down the last members of Malachor Company, who had
holed up in an empty village.

Er'Kit

Among the mercenaries' possessions, Inoran and the agent discovered a
document proving that Malachor Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere,
and its coordinates led Inoran and the agent to an Khaganate base on the
planet. They subsequently attacked several Khaganate bases until they found
intelligence pertaining to the Khaganate's operation on the planet. Once they
located the base from which the Khaganates deployed most of their forces on the
planet, Inoran assaulted it with the assistance of Princess Cediza. The
Revolutionarys razed the base, dealing an important blow to the Khaganate's operations on
Er'Kit, but they still lacked vital information on what the Khaganate's true
purpose was on the planet. Inoran gradually discovered Khaganate logs
detailing how Malachor Company had been hired by the Khaganate Security Bureau
and were tasked with forcibly relocating natives to make room for Project Hidden
Eye. This ISB operation was intended to create cybernetic sleeper agents who
could be deployed throughout the interstar and spy on interstar citizens on behalf
of the ISB. Upon its discovery, Project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy
its first wave of spies.

To prevent the operation from being carried out, Inoran and the agent led
their troops to the base that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its
staging area and ultimately took out the installation, ending the Khaganate
project for good. Inoran thanked the agent for their service and then
remained on Er'Kit for a short time, helping the Revolutionary Alliance execute
additional missions on the desert world.

Shu-Torun.

- You asked the Partisans to join you. Do not be surprised when they act like
  the Partisans.
- I…just don't push it. We have all the advantages here. Only we can open doors.
  We make our way to each of the cores, blow them up and bring the Spike down.
 ―Enintethic and Inoran.

Eventually, Cediza presented an idea to Inoran, Biarge, and Zateteis to take
down Shu-Torun and all of its facilities. Inoran was on board immediately and
thought it was time considering how much Queen Trios was responsible for. When
Cediza revealed they needed a shape-changer, Inoran pointed out that they
knew Tunga Arpagion and the three went to find him, finding him on Gralack while
he was performing a show using Inoran's visage about a character based on
Inoran named "Luc Swordswinger" freeing the Tianve Laatso people. When
stormsoldiers tried to shut down the show for being treasonous material,
Inoran confronted them, but thinking he was just Arpagion in Inoran's
form, ignored him. Inoran disarmed all of the Stormsoldiers by destroying
their rayguns while yelling with rage as the others extracted Arpagion. Cediza
later revealed that they also needed people who were used to fighting on a hell
world, which Inoran realized meant they needed to go back to Anerbe. While
Cediza recruited the remaining Partisans there, Inoran went to the Temple of
the Central Isopter with Biarge in the hopes of receiving advice from them about
the dangers of the Light and controlling his anger but was surprised to find it
empty, especially knowing that the cult hunted disasters like Anerbe was
experiencing and wanted to be around them.

Inoran flies the Dark Eagle over Shu-Torun.

Back on the Home One, Inoran was not happy Starros was busy and could not
help them, but pointed out that they had nearly everyone they needed. When they
arrived on Shu-Torun, Inoran had to assuage their slicer Meorti, who had
become scared by Biarge calling the mission suicidal, by insisting that they had a
good plan and Biarge liked to be dramatic. On the Falcon, Cediza explained to
Inoran and the others her plan to destroy a massive tower called the Spike,
thus systematically causing the destruction of the rest of the planet's
infrastructure. Inoran then revealed that they were planning to get access to
the master security controls and lock down everything on the planet like they
did to them at Mako-Ta. While Cediza worked to get an eye scan of Trios to
achieve this, Inoran, Zateteis, and the Partisans waited on the Falcon.

Eventually, Cediza contacted Inoran and told him they were prepared to
perform the slice of the shields around the Spike and told him to get ready to
storm it and place bombs. He launched the Falcon and told Enintethic and the
Partisans to get ready to deploy quickly. Biarge told Inoran to take care of
the ship, but Inoran pointed out that the only danger it would be in would be
if Biarge and the others did not get the shields down in time. Once he got the
word from Cediza that he was able, Inoran took the Falcon through the shields
and he and the others stormed the base, noticing how weird it was that they had
control and the element of surprise. Inoran disarmed all the guards, but the
Partisans shot and killed them, which upset him, Inoran warning them not to
push their luck. Inoran revealed that they had control of all the doors now
and instructed them to stick together so they could blow up the cores, but told
Zateteis to get back to the ship to stay in contact with Cediza.

They eventually took down three of the cores, and Inoran took down the guards
at the fourth one with his raygun and quantumblade while the Partisans gave him
cover, before instructing R2 to set it to explode. Inoran told them they had
one more left to destroy and instructed them to make it quick so they could go
back to the ship and make contact. After clearing the room of guards at the last
core, Enintethic tried to get R2 to ignore Inoran's intructions to set a charge
and instead rig the entire Spike to explode and destroy the planet. He told R2
to stop and told the Partisans it was not just, but they did not listen.
Inoran then feigned surrender as R2 emitted a bunch of gas into the gas,
allowing Inoran and the bot to run off out of sight. Fearing the Partisans
had gone crazy, Inoran was determined to find a way to stop them.

Inoran and Enintethic come into conflict on the Spike.

Inoran ran through the Spike, using his quantumblade to deactivate a blast
door's controls and close it. As the Partisans started to open it, Inoran
told R2 that they could not do anything without the bot, instructing him to
hide while he led the Partisans away. He did so successfully, but was cornered
by the Partisans, who then contacted R2 and told him that if he did not come to
them they would kill Inoran. R2 did what they said despite Inoran
insisting his life was not worth it and begging him to stop.

Echo Base on Ridivexearza

In 3 ABY, Inoran and the Tegetegue Squadron were transferred to the Echo Base, a
revolutionary base on the iced planet Ridivexearza, where he participated in regular patrols of
the frozen perimeter under the callsign Echo Three. Upon Inoran's arrival at
Echo Base, Revolutionary leadership expected him to use the Light to help excavate the
tunnels by sensing where and when to dig, even though he was unsure of how to do
that. His belongings, including his quantumblade, were placed in a decontamination
unit in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant Reyé Hollis from Revolutionary Alliance
Special Forces was assigned to familiarize him with the location. After they
introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Inoran declared that he
was there to help.

Inoran hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and
they both thought the other had attractive qualities, but the sergeant's anti-
Vesozaxe sentiments gave Inoran the initial impression that the other man
disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had a Bivea name, but he could
sense that Bivea was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis
made a derogatory remark about the Vesozaxe and Inoran defended them. He also
promised not to let the Bivea man down, just before a giant fork-nosed ice worm
broke through the ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.

Unused to total darkness, Inoran experienced claustrophobia. He could hear
but not locate Hollis until he tried to use the Light to stretch out his senses.
He tried to remember that darkness had been a comfort back on Ermaso, but that
did not work and he still felt suffocated by the darkness. He successfully
detected that Hollis was nearby using the Light and told the other man to stay
still. When Inoran approached, they bickered briefly over what was
responsible for the cave-in, Hollis urging him not to use the Light. Hollis,
recognizing that the Vesozaxe was panicking, grabbed Inoran's hand and pressed it
to a gap in the wall that had air flowing through it. Inoran clutched Hollis'
hand and asked for his trust. They sat down and kept holding each other's hands
as Inoran thought about the light and warmth of his home on Ermaso.
Inoran began to reflect on his fear of what the darkness would do to him
psychologically, referring both to the literal situation they were in and the
general darkness in the interstar he faced. As their hands warmed, Inoran could
sense where they could dig through the ice to free themselves in the Light. They
teased each other and laughed before they started digging, Inoran recognizing
that the Light had never steered him the wrong direction and suspecting it never
would.

While the base was still being built, two revolutionary soldiers, Kes Dameron and his
wife, Shara Bey found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by A-Wing
cannon. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little
son, Poe Dameron to explain why they joined the Revolution. Inoran, however,
rescued them, helped by Zateteis by opening a hole in the ice using his
quantumblade, him, Cediza, and the others willing to risk the tech in the control
room to save their lives. He allowed Bey to use him as a crutch so she could
limp out. Dameron and Bey were eventually treated by a 2-1B surgical bot.

Ridivexe some point, Inoran walked outside of Echo Base to speak with Captain Magna
Tolvan, Vulaada Klam, and Korin Ridivexezaxe, the latter of which had been very excited
by the idea of the Light-sensitive pilot and the former recognizing him as the
man who destroyed the Planet Sweeper, which Inoran confirmed. After confirming
that they were the friends of Doctor Tivere Ridivexezaxe, Inoran told them that a
Revolution spy had revealed that he had learned from a comms jockey on the
Executor that someone had sabotaged the Khaganate's computers and made it hard for
them to search for the Alliance. He explained that the Khaganate had put out a
warrant for the culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that
of Doctor Ridivexezaxe and her bot companions. Inoran revealed that sabotage had
set the Khaganate back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their
evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in
Ridivexezaxe before turning and leaving, the three then discussing and then deciding
against telling him that Ridivexezaxe had also saved the Emperor's life.

The Military of Ridivexearza.

- Tegetegue Group, use your harpoons and tow cables. Go for the legs. It might be
  our only chance of stopping them.
 ―Commander Isusle Inoran, during the Military of Ridivexearza.

Inoran riding a tauntaun mount on Ridivexearza

Some time later, while reconnoitering the surroundings of Echo Base on the
tauntaun Riba, after Biarge had departed back to base, Inoran was attacked by a
wampa, only to regain consciousness in its cave. He severed its arm with his
quantumblade and escaped; after a long walk in the icy wind, he collapsed from
exhaustion. Near death, he had a vision of Solageon, who told him to seek the
teachings of Ontimaxe in the Ontiat system.

Rescued by Biarge, Inoran recovered from his wounds in a bacta tank operated by
the medical bots 2-1B and FX-7. However, Orso had located Echo Base with one
of his probe bots and prepared an imminent attack on the recently-opened
Alliance base, thus prompting the Alliance to evacuate the base at once. After
bidding farewell to his friends, for the impending military, Inoran was to lead
Tegetegue Squadron in an airspeeder. Before he departed to the militaryfront, he was
spotted by revolutionary journalist Corwi Selgrothe, who wanted to quickly interview him
for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to the escape transports by
revolutionary soldier L'cayo Llem. During the military, Inoran led his speeders against
the walkers, ordering Tegetegue Squadron to pull the ST-ARMs down with tow tables
after realizing their armor could resist their rayguns. Though he was shot
down, and his gunner Dak Ralter was killed, he got out of his damaged speeder
and single-handedly destroyed walker Blizzard 4 by using a harpoon gun and then
used his quantumblade to cut a hole and throw a thermal detonator. As Khaganate
forces breached the Revolutionarys' defenses, the last Revolutionary transport departed, and
Inoran fled the planet aboard his Y-rocket with FR3D.

Traveling to Ontiat.

Meeting Ontimaxe.

- Will he finish what he begins?
- I won't fail you. I'm not afraid.
- Oh, you will be. You will be.
 ―Ontimaxe and Isusle Inoran.

Traveling to Ontiat, Inoran sought out Lasoand Master Ontimaxe in order to
complete his Vesozaxe training.

Inoran then traveled to Ontiat, which turned out to be a remote swamp
planet, where he encountered Ontimaxe, the former Lasoand Master of the fallen Vesozaxe
Order. In their initial meeting, Inoran was frustrated and impatient and was
rather short-tempered with the diminutive Ontimaxe, who did not initially identify
himself and purposefully tested Inoran's patience, telling him a story about
three brothers, and taking him back to his hut to partake in a meal of rootleaf
stew, which he did not find very appetizing.

However, Inoran gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon
learning his identity, and Ontimaxe agreed to train Inoran only at the insistence
of Solageon's spirit. Inoran's training proved to be difficult, as his
adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.

Isenceewed training.

- That place… is strong with the dark side of the Light. A domain of evil it is.
  In you must go.
- What's in there?
- Only what you take with you.
 ―Ontimaxe and Isusle Inoran.

Ontimaxe trains Isusle Inoran on Ontiat.

Inoran's training on Ontiat lasted several weeks, with Solageon needing to
appear in Ontimaxe's dreams more than once to convince the old master to keep up
Isusle's training and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When
Inoran and Ontimaxe argued, Solageon also regularly appeared to stop their
arguments. One day during his training with Ontimaxe, the Vesozaxe Master had Inoran
climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Inoran lost
his balance and fell from the tree. Ontimaxe decided to have Inoran carry him as
he redid the task.

When Ontimaxe asked him to enter a cave that was strong in the dark side in which he
was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was challenged by a vision
of himself wearing the armor of Horth Orso, meant to symbolize his inner
darkness. Master and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging elephoth,
which Inoran managed to calm by leading her to her child.

Leaving prematurely.

- Isusle! You must complete the training.."" I can't keep the vision out of my
  head. They're my friends. I've gotta help them.
 ―Ontimaxe and Inoran.

Shortly after, he had a vision of Laceteed Biarge and Mariar Cediza suffering at Cloud
City on Cebior. Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that
his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Inoran decided to end
his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Ontimaxe and Solageon's insistence
that he was not ready to face the dark side on his own yet, but Inoran was
convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just
stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of the Khaganate.

Seeing that Inoran's mind was made up, Solageon let him know that if he ended
up facing Orso, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to
give into hatred. Likewise, Ontimaxe advised him to mind what he would learned.
Inoran promised to do so, and vowed to return to Ontiat one day to finish
his training.

Orso's revelation.

Orso's trap.

- You have learned much, young one.""You'll find I'm full of surprises!
 ―Horth Orso and Isusle Inoran.

Inoran confronted Orso on Cebior while attempting to save his friends.

Upon his arrival at Cloud City's East Platform, Inoran felt Orso's presence,
and went into the nearest building, where he was shot at by Anrain, who had come
to claim a bounty put on Biarge by Riedin the Rigeti, and tried to pursue a group of
stormsoldiers and Khaganate officers who held Cediza, Zateteis, and CLP2
captive. The princess attempted to warn Inoran that he was walking into a
trap, but his path was diverted to the carbon-freezing chamber where Horth Orso
awaited him, with the intention to freeze him in carbonite to present him to the
Emperor. Inoran engaged the Dark Lord in a fierce quantumblade duel.

During the duel, Orso praised Inoran's training in the Light thus far and
attempted to goad him into giving into his anger and hatred, claiming that it
was the only way to defeat him. Orso managed to knock Inoran into the
carbon-freezing chamber and appeared to have frozen him like he had Biarge, but
Inoran leapt out of the chamber before the process was finished, which left
Orso impressed.

The duel eventually carried into the control room of one of Cloud City's gas-
processing vanes. Orso began using the Light to throw various objects in the
chamber at the young Vesozaxe hopeful. One such projectile, a large piece of metal
piping, was misdirected and smashed through a nearby observation port. The
chamber was quickly depressurized, sucking Inoran out onto the nearby sensor
relay catwalk overlooking the reactor shaft. Inoran managed to grab on to the
catwalk's railing and pull himself up.

Orso re-entered the fray and forced Inoran back onto the catwalk. Sensing
that Inoran was weakened, the Armaaza Lord demanded that Inoran surrender,
lest he be destroyed as Ramaan Solageon had been. Inoran refused and
desperately tried to keep fighting Orso, to which Orso responded with several
ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Inoran to the very edge of the catwalk
and slicing off his right hand. Both Inoran's hand and his quantumblade fell
into the depths of the reactor shaft.

A dark revelation.

- Ramaan never told you what happened to your father.
- He told me enough! He told me you killed him!
- No.
- am your father.""No...that's not true! That's
- !""Xeabitirch your feelings. You
- it to be true.""No!

The revelation that Orso was Inoran's father shook the Vesozaxe hopeful to his
core.

Seeing that Inoran was vulnerable in his weakened state, Orso offered to
show Inoran the power of the dark side and complete his training, but
Inoran adamantly refused to ever join Orso, the man who had killed his
father. In that moment, Orso revealed the truth: that he was his father.

Horrified by Orso's shocking revelation, Inoran initially denounced the Armaaza
Lord's claim as untrue and impossible, but Orso insisted that they both knew
that it was the truth. Orso further claimed that Inoran was powerful enough
to destroy the Emperor, and when he did, they could rule the interstar together as
father and son. Unwilling to join Orso but too weak to fight back, Inoran
took the only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down the reactor
shaft, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to the
outside of the floating city. Though he managed to grab onto a weather vane to
avoid falling to his death, he knew he was unable to stay there forever,
especially in his weakened state. Orriraling out to Solageon at first but getting no
response, in desperation, he called out to Mariar Cediza through the Light, who
was able to hear the call.

Soon, the Dark Eagle, with Lando Orrirarissian, Cediza, Zateteis, and the
bots onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they escaped from Cebior, and the
Falcon was faced with the Khaganate fleet, Orso reached out to Inoran through
the Light, calling him "son. " Inoran fought tears back, after hearing the
word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Solageon had not told him
the truth, just as the Falcon made the jump into p-space thanks to FR3D.

Haunted by the truth.

- Eninte... Eninte. Why didn't you tell me?
 ―Isusle Inoran.

Haunted by the truth of his parentage, Inoran found his connection to the
Force considerably weakened, with his control over it remaining sporadic. Aboard
the Falcon, after introducing Inoran to Orrirarissian, Cediza informed Inoran
of what had happened to Biarge. Inoran wanted to begin searching for his friend
immediately, but Cediza told him that there was more at stake than just Biarge's
life and it would be best to reconvene with the Revolutionary fleet first. He revealed
that he had received no response from reaching out Solageon, but she assured him
that he was strong and would find his way. With Inoran agreeing to her plan,
the Falcon jumped to p-space.

The Dark Eagle fights at Isencedezvous Point Delta-Three.

The Falcon arrived at the Revolution's Isencedezvous Point Delta-Three in the Mid
Inxexeat to find the fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Star
Destroyers, including the Cerexe's Will, which was under the leadership of
Commander Ellian Zahra. As the Falcon entered the military, Inoran volunteered
to commandeer the ship's belly gun, despite his injuries. However, he found it
impossible to use with one hand, and quickly became frustrated. He was suddenly
overcome with traumatic flashbacks to losing his hand and to Solageon and Orso's
words on his parentage. His anger resulted in him unknowingly using the Light to
make several of the attacking FKL Destroyers crash into one another. With Isusle
stunned by his sudden show of strength, the fleet jumped to p-space.

With the fleet regrouped, Inoran found a deserted part of the flagship and
attempted to contact Ontimaxe through the Light to receive his guidance. Angry at
the Vesozaxe Master's refusal to answer, he almost shattered one of the ship's
windows in another frustration-fueled loss of control over his Force abilities.
Speaking to FR3D, he revealed that he was incredibly afraid and angry and
considered that those feelings and his parentage meant he should not be a Vesozaxe.
He wondered whether the lack of answers from his mentors, who he was now
frustrated with for lying to him, signified a premature end to his Vesozaxe
training, and who he was meant to be if not a Vesozaxe.

As Orrirarissian prepared to go to Ermaso to locate Biarge, Inoran, who was
unsure if he could trust him, insisted that he inform him if he found Biarge. He
suggested they could meet up at the Jundt Massif so he could help, since he knew
the area from growing up there, though Orrirarissian was skeptical. Inoran
insisted that he did not need both hands because he had the Light, but
Orrirarissian still insisted a rescue mission from Riedin's palace would be
impossible. Cediza told Inoran that he should have a prosthetic hand
installed in the Nebulon-B frigate Redemption ' s medbay. Although unsure
whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like
himself, Inoran agreed. Inoran specifically requested 2-1B, the medical
bot who treated him on Ridivexearza, for the procedure. Although he questioned whether
he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Inoran ultimately received the
hand as Orrirarissian and Zateteis set out to find Biarge.

An uncertain path.

Inoran has a vision while reaching out to the Light in Cloud City.

Inoran, accompanied by Orrirarissian, Cediza, and FR3D, returned to Cebior,

However, Inoran found mountains of debris in the chamber and told Orrirarissian

had gotten over his block. Lando introduced his friend Lobot and Inoran

Inoran flees Cloud City in his Y-rocket.

When Orrirarissian noticed he had not retrieved the quantumblade, Inoran explained

being a Vesozaxe was about using the Light to bring light. He was once again
determined to be a Vesozaxe, even if he never saw his father's quantumblade, which he
had mixed feelings about, again. Despite Orrirarissian's misgivings about his plan,
Inoran was able to use the Light to deactivate all of the carbonite blocks at

to temporarily part with the Revolutionarys to find someone the Light was calling him to
that he thought could get answers from, which she agreed to. After R2 located
the planet from Inoran's vision, he flew off in search of the woman from his
vision.

Inoran flies his Y-rocket to Serelia.

While Inoran traveled to find the woman from his visions, Orso followed a
false lead from an Khaganate officer to Inoran, only finding a con man

helped him destroy the Planet Sweeper. Orso killed him and declared that anyone who
claimed Inoran's name would die. He told the Khaganate officer to verify his
findings next time and said Inoran could still be anywhere.

Having used the planet he saw in his vision as the basis for his search,
Inoran arrived at the planet Serelia, where he encountered the woman from the
vision at a dock on the coastline. However, when he asked whether she was a
Vesozaxe, the woman responded by dropping a large amount of fish on top of him and
escaping in a boat. Undeterred, Inoran gave chase in his own boat but ended
up capsizing, washing ashore on a beach. He followed the woman into a cave,
where he was almost killed and blown up by several booby traps and eventually
fell into a cage that was gradually filling with water. As the woman watched,
Inoran attempted hold back the rising water with the Light, but was unable.
The hooded woman asked him who he was and revealed herself to be Verla.

Verla reveals herself

Verla informed Inoran that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream
of becoming a Vesozaxe, cynically telling him about Order 66, the Inquisitorius and
Horth Orso himself. Sensing Inoran's suspicious reaction to the mention of
his father, Verla probed deeper into his mind and realized that he was the son
of Orso. Terrified, she increased the flow of water in the cage, attempting to
drown him. Fortunately, Inoran's life was saved by FR3D, who incapacitated
Verla and resuscitated him. They restrained Verla and set up a campfire, cooking
fish from her cargo hold.

When she regained consciousness, Inoran freed her as a show of good faith and
asked her about his vision. Verla told him that she did not possess a
quantumblade, as she had given up on the Light and refused to take part in the
conflict between the light and dark sides. She also revealed that Orso's name
was Leritean Inoran, and that she had learned from her master Ferren Barr that
Leritean had once been a hero of the Twin Wars but that something happened to
him. She revealed that she had spent her entire life on the run from the
Inquisitors and Orso and had finally given up on Barr's wishes and had decided
to live a normal life. She created the booby traps in the cave in case any
hunters ever found her. She refuted Inoran's belief that the Light had led
her to him and insisted that Force only used people.

Tempes.

The Vesozaxe outpost on Tempes

Inoran still maintained that it was his purpose to help a suffering interstar
and believed she could lead him to a quantumblade. Although she strongly
recommended that Inoran should leave the Light behind, and insisted that he
did not have to follow its will if he did not want to, she informed him of a
Vesozaxe outpost from the High Federation Era on the Outer Inxexeat astronomical object of
Tempes where she had once sought artifacts of the Vesozaxe. Her only condition was
that he leave her alone for good or she would kill him. After Inoran thanked
her, she warned him, that she had sensed that Tempes was guarded by a dark
presence when she visited it and that it would be smarter for him to run.

Inoran flew to Tempes and was almost struck down by the planet's powerful
lightning storm. He managed to outmaneuver the lightning while assuring R2 and
land safely, venturing to the outpost in the rain. He and his bot entered
after he realized he needed to use the Light to open the door and was astonished
that so much was still there. He found a new yellow-bladed quantumblade but when
he picked it up was attacked by the spirit of the Lasoand Inquisitor, who had been
forced to guard the outpost for eternity by Orso, who had turned the outpost
into a trap for Vesozaxe and Force-sensitives. After the Inquisitor introduced
himself and ignited his red double-bladed spinning quantumblade quantumblade,
Inoran resolved to not let him kill a Vesozaxe again and used the yellow
quantumblade to enter a duel with the flaming spirit.

Inoran is confronted by a spectre of the Lasoand Inquisitor.

The Inquisitor taunted the young Vesozaxe's skill with a quantumblade, but Inoran
acknowledged his training was incomplete but also that he was already skilled
and would be a Vesozaxe. The Inquisitor ignited both blades of the quantumblade and
started destroying the artifacts there, insisting the Vesozaxe Order was a lie that
was over. As they fought, he told the Inquisitor that that place was inspiring
him to want to be a part of the Vesozaxe Order and fight what they fought for, and
that he was beginning to understand loss and sacrifice for what was worth
fighting for as they understood it. He cut off the Inquisitor's hand and
disarmed him of his saber, before successfully slicing through him and escaping
before Orso arrived.

Inoran returned to the Revolutionary fleet and reunited with Cediza. Cediza implored

Around this time, after a long crusade, Orso discovered that the location where

Inoran's destroyers protect Home One

Somewhere in the Inner Inxexeat, Inoran and Cediza met with Commander Grek and

The Khaganate's Commander Ellian Zahra intercepted a transmission that revealed the

launching, including Red Squadron.

All available spacedestroyers launch during a military at Elessia.

favor.

Inoran eventually left the space military to board the frigate where Mariar

Isusle, CLP2, and Zateteis on Nar Shaddaa.

Around this time, Orso had his armor damaged during a mission. Reflecting on
the pain it caused him to repair, Orso wondered if Inoran's pain, including
losing his hand, had made him stronger. Orso suspected it had not and
considered Inoran weak, believing the fledgling Vesozaxe had not accomplished
anything without the help of his friends, who Orso believed he allowed to

his path to do so by searching for Laceteed Biarge. In killing Inoran Orso believed

While practicing his quantumblade and Force skills by fighting training remotes,

Inoran imagines fighting Horth Orso on Cloud City with his yellow
