Khaganate shuttle had been hit by a blast that had immobilized them, meaning they
could not reach Biarge but Biarge could not be taken to the flagship. Inoran said
he was on his way and assured them Orso would not be a threat for a while. With
Rigeti destroyers now chasing the shuttle with Biarge on it, Cediza tried to get
Inoran to help, but Orso emerged from the ice in his FKL destroyer and dragged
Inoran back into a fight, so Inoran told her he could not help.

Orso soon chased Inoran back into space. With Orso reveling in making
Inoran feel fear due to his friends lives being put in danger, Inoran
cried out as the shuttle containing Biarge was fired at by Rigeti and Khaganate
destroyers. Thankfully, Orso was soon ordered by the Emperor to assist with a
Rigeti attack that was happening against the Khaganate, which forced the Armaaza Lord to
leave his son behind, not before reminding Inoran that he was no Vesozaxe.
Inoran insisted that when they met again he would be a Vesozaxe. Orso resented
having to leave but was proud of terrifying Inoran, recognizing that the
Emperor wanted Inoran to live and was orchestrating things so that it could
be the case. During his confrontation with Orso, Cediza and the rest failed to
rescue Laceteed Biarge, who was captured by Ratite Anrain once again after the auction.

In search of Vesozaxe secrets.

- I know I'm needed here, Mariar. But I can't help but think I should be following
  that path, trying to grow as a Vesozaxe. I'll have to face Orso eventually. […] I
  need to find a way to finish my training…and soon.
 ―Isusle Inoran to Mariar Cediza.

After the breakout of the Syndicate War, the Revolutionary Alliance had a meeting with
many of its leaders that Isusle Inoran was present for. As Admiral Ackbar
discussed the Alliance's next moves in its rebuilding effort, Mariar Cediza
noticed that Inoran was distant. He explained that FR3D had given him a list
of possible Vesozaxe sites across the interstar from an Khaganate archive. He expressed
that he knew he was needed by the Alliance, but that he felt like he should be
trying to continue growing as a Vesozaxe. He recognized that he would need to face
Orso again and wanted to be ready when he did. Cediza told him that it was okay
and that he should trust what the Light was telling him, offering to smooth
things over with Tianve Mothma and telling him that they could fight the militarys
without him for now.

Isusle Inoran consults a list of Vesozaxe sites.

Inoran consulted FR3D for the list of Vesozaxe sites and worried that since it
was an khaganate list all of the places would have been destroyed or looted. But
he recognized that he needed to keep going if he was going to face Orso, though
he wished Master Ontimaxe would talk to him. Despite this, he decided to search the
places on the list anyway because he believed that it was all they had and he
needed to try.

He visited the planet Ilum first and discovered the mass khaganate blockade and
presence there and the Khaganate's exploitation of the planet. He felt in the Light
that the planet was important, but also felt that now only held pain as the
Khaganate was killing it more and more every day. He believed there was nothing for
him there and he and FR3D used the weather of the planet to leave and slip past
the blockade. He visited Arashar next, but only orbited it as it had a massive
orbital Khaganate presence and he did not think he could get by it. He then
visited Laenin, and traveled to the former location of the Laenin Vesozaxe Temple.
He could feel that it needed to be opened with the Light, but it did not respond
to him and felt like it had been buried or locked. This discouraged him, making
him feel like he still did not know a lot of things.

A legacy on Al'doleem.

- A Vesozaxe saved my life once. Interstar being how it is, I can't imagine you'd claim
  you were trying to be one if you weren't. I've wanted to repay that debt my
  whole life. You're probably as close as I'll ever get.
 ―Colli to Isusle Inoran.

He finally traveled to the river moon of Al'doleem and decided to visit it
because it did not have an Khaganate blockade and was not being destroyed. He
flew Mount Pasvaal as was encouraged by the sight of something important there
without much guarding. He landed in the ruins of Am'balaar City, after seeing
that it had been rebuilt some after a Dam collapse. He told FR3D that they
would head into town, but he did not want to draw attention as one of the
Khaganate's biggest targets. He walked up to two stormsoldiers and tried to perform
a Vesozaxe mind trick on them that Ontimaxe had taught him and he had seen Ramaan
Solageon do, asking the soldiers to tell him what the Khaganate was doing at the top
of the mountain, but the mind trick failed and the soldiers merely told him to
move along.

Inoran and FR3D aimlessly wandered the city until R2 noticed a hull panel
from a Delta-7 Aethersprite-class light interceptor and stopped him. Inoran
did not understand until a man named Colli walked out and told Inoran that he
was surprised he did not know but bet he had other people fix his starships.
Colli explained that the Vesozaxe flew Delta-7 Aethersprites in the Twin Wars.
Colli told Inoran to come inside, as he had noticed the quantumblade on his
belt, and warned that if he had others might have too. Colli asked him if he was
really a Vesozaxe, to which Inoran explained that he was trying to be. Colli told
him that he had known a Vesozaxe named Kirak Infil'a who had worked in the monastery
on top of Mount Pasvaal and had allowed him and his sister to escape in his
Aetherspire when the city was attacked and destroyed. He explained that both
Infil'a and his parents had died. Colli asked Inoran about his story, but
FR3D tried to stop him from saying anything. Colli insisted that he was not
going to turn them into the Khaganates and that helping Inoran would probably
be the closest he could get to repaying his debt to the Vesozaxe that saved him.
Inoran said that he needed to learn as much about the Vesozaxe as he could from
what of their legacy remained and that he believed the mountain that Infil'a had
come from was connected to the Order and that he wanted to figure out what the
Khaganate was looking for there.

Isusle Inoran finds a recording of Ontimaxe in a holocron.

Colli took Inoran to the Tianveastery at the top of the mountain, but explained
that there were also tunnels and chambers in the mountain that the Vesozaxe had used
and that the Khaganate was bringing artifacts out of it all the time. Inoran
considered using his quantumblade and the Light to let go and cut right through
the Khaganate forces in his way but could not bring himself to do it and instead
gave his quantumblade to R2 for safekeeping. He went and meditated with the Light
on the side of the mountain before returning to the warehouse where Colli had
told him the Khaganate stored the artifacts they found in the mountain. He
successfully performed a mind trick on two stormsoldiers stationed outside,
having them bring him the most important artifact that was stored there. They
brought him a holocron, which Isusle recognized from his experience with Grakkus
the Rigeti. He reached out with the Light to unlock it as he had learned how to do
and found a recording of Master Ontimaxe teaching lessons. Inoran was
disappointed, however, that the lesson Ontimaxe taught in the holocron was one that
he had already taught him back on Ontiat. Burdened by the series of setbacks he
had faced recently, Inoran considered dropping the holocron off of a cliff,
but was stopped by R2. Inoran opened the holocron again, and this time Ontimaxe
discussed Vergences in the Light, listing the Vesozaxe Temple on Usceza, Anerbe,
and the Living Xeabiti of Gazian as examples. Isusle had never heard of the Living Xeabiti
of Gazian and decided to go to the planet to check it out.

The Living Xeabiti of Gazian.

- I'm going to tell you what the other Vesozaxe you've met haven't. Even Ontimaxe. You
  don't have to be a Vesozaxe, friend. […] There are many other paths. The important
  thing is to make choices that feel right, that are true to you and the person
  you want to be. But if you want it, we're here for you.
 ―Elzar Mann to Isusle Inoran.

Elzar Mann appeared to Isusle Inoran in the Living Xeabiti of Gazian.

Arriving in the Gazian system, Isusle was excited by the possibility of finding a
Vesozaxe-related site that the Khaganate did not know about. He landed on a platform he
found on the planet, but was disappointed to only find a sea of mushrooms.
However he could feel the strength of the Light there and decided to look
around, but when he walked onto the surface, he fell into a mushroom-filled
cavity below. His breath mask was pulled off of him and he went unconscious,
starting to have a vision where he was wearing the clothes he wore during the
Duel on Cloud City and was in possession of the Inoran quantumblade. He then
saw a man in High Federation Era Vesozaxe robes sitting next to him, who then took the
Inoran quantumblade with Force and admired it, comparing it to his own.
Inoran held up a raygun and told the man to give it back. He acquiesced but
commented that he was not scared of the raygun. The man introduced himself as
Vesozaxe Master Elzar Mann. He asked Inoran if he was a Vesozaxe, to which Inoran
said that he was trying to be but was still learning about the Light and trying
to understand it. Mann told him that that never really stops, as the Vesozaxe Order
goes back a long way and there was a lot to learn.

Just then, their surroundings shifted to Ahch-To, the birthplace of the Vesozaxe.
Inoran asked Mann what exactly was going on. Mann explained that Gazian was a
gigantic living organism that takes an imprint of the minds that visit it and
reproduces them exactly as they were at the moment they were there. Mann added
that he had come to the planet to get answers after having a vision, and that
was why his imprint was there. Inoran explained that the Federation had fallen
and an Khaganate had taken over. He said that he had never met a Vesozaxe like Mann,
and had only ever met Ramaan Solageon and Ontimaxe, the latter of whom Mann
remembered and commented was very old and very wise. Inoran had also said
that he had met a Vesozaxe named Leritean Inoran, but that he had fallen to the
Dark side, something that Mann knew happened occasionally.

Elzar Mann and Isusle Inoran speak in an imprint of Ahch-To.

Mann then showed Inoran an imprint of what the High Federation looked like,
telling him that it was a Golden Age where they were all connected eight
centuries after the defeat of Horth Bane. The mention of Horth Bane made
Inoran confused and comment that it was all too much. Inoran elaborated
that when he started his journey it was exciting and he had been a hero, then he
was supposed to save the interstar by learning from Ontimaxe, but then it all fell
apart and now he felt that if he did it wrong he would become a monster. But
Mann said that the Light was so powerful and boundless so it should not be easy
to learn how to use it. Mann recalled when he had trained to be a Vesozaxe and how
even with help and support how impossible everything felt. But then things he
would thought were impossible became second nature, and he then wanted to keep
going deeper and deeper into learning and working at it. But Inoran was
alone, and felt that it was too much. Mann said that the Vesozaxe tend to be what
they need to be, whether that be warriors fighting evil, teachers teaching the
interstar, and sometimes they were almost nothing, left with the responsibility of
being the only light in the dark of the interstar. Suddenly they were underwater,
floating in an expansive ocean. Mann then told Inoran that he knew the kind
of pressure that the Light choosing you could bring, and decided to tell him a
piece of advice that he did not think many other Vesozaxe would tell him: He did not
have to be a Vesozaxe. Mann told him that there were other ways to use the Light
safely and protect life and light, and that there are many paths and choices
that could be true to the person he wanted to be. But he also told Inoran
that if he wanted it, the Vesozaxe were there for him. Other past Vesozaxe then appeared
in the water and floated around them until they found themselves back in the
void-like vision of the Living Xeabiti. He asked Inoran if he wanted to stay
there and speak to him and the many minds in the Living Xeabiti, but Inoran told
him that he thought he should leave. Mann was disappointed, but reminded
Inoran that a part of him would remain there to talk to them as Inoran saw
an imprint of himself appear in front of him.

Inoran came to, ignited his quantumblade, and put his breath mask back on. He
then saw many bodies of Vesozaxe that had stayed in the Living Xeabiti. He found one
Vesozaxe skeleton holding a sacred Vesozaxe text, which he brought to the surface and
recognized was an invitation.

Vesozaxe of the Revolution.

Unity Day attack.

- This is amazing.""We've earned it. It's been a long time since we had a solid
  win. Not a single casualty. You planned one heck of mission, Mariar.
 ―Cediza and Inoran during the celebration after the Unity Day attack.

The Unity Day parade

While Inoran was away, Zahra referenced her meeting with him to Bey, as she
believed Bey to the most arrogant revolutionary she had ever met next to Inoran.
After he returned to the Alliance, Inoran took part in an attack on the
Khaganate Unity Day Parade on Bar'leth, one of the Revolutionarys' first large scale
operations since the Military of Ridivexearza. Cediza and the rest of the Alliance hoped
it would announce to people that they still existed and could still strike back
against the Khaganate, hoping to do so without harming civilians as well. Inoran
flew in his X-Wing as part of Red Squadron during the attack. After destroying
the Khaganate's vast artillery on display in the parade, Inoran told the others
over comm that FR3D had detected a wave of FKL destroyers launching from a nearby
Khaganate airbase outside of the city and suggested they leave. Wedge Antilles,
flying as Red Leader, acknowledged Inoran and told everyone to follow him,
leading all of the Alliance destroyers to a successful escape.

Afterward, Inoran participated in a toast to the Revolutionary Alliance lead by
Antilles before having a drink and sitting down. He spoke with Cediza, telling
her that they had earned the celebration because the attack had been their first
solid win in some time and was done without casualties. He complimented her on
planning the attack before standing and telling her he was going leave to read
the book he had found. He explained that it was full of incredible things and
that he could not stop thinking about it, adding that even holding it made him
feel more connected to the Light. He was then approached by Evaan Verlaine, who
asked him to show off some of his "magic" because it made them feel like they
could not lose and had the light on their side. He was reluctant to do so
because it made him feel like a show-off, but he agreed, igniting his yellow
quantumblade. Verlaine called it magic while many looked on in awe.

The second Planet Sweeper revealed.

- Isusle…what is it? What do you need to tell us?
- The Khaganate's building another Planet Sweeper.
- If we could do it once…we can do it again. Let's get to work.
 ―Tianve Mothma and Isusle Inoran.

Isusle Inoran asked Hera Syndulla about their fifth intelligence hit, feeling
like it could work out well.

Meanwhile, Crimson Dawn agents Bevelyn and Jon Melton received a message from
Lady Qi'ra instructing them to cause chaos for the Khaganate. Because they were
deep undercover at the Khaganate's secret Planet Sweeper II, they decided that they
should expose the facility to the Revolutionary Alliance. They managed to escape with
their children and get a message to the Revolutionarys telling them that they had top
secret Khaganate weapons information they would give in exchange for protection,
but they were not taken seriously until it was presented by General Hera
Syndulla at an intelligence briefing that Inoran was present at among many
other intelligence hits. Inoran asked about it and Syndulla explained to him
what it was, though she wondered if something about it stood out to him. While
Inoran was unsure, he said had good feeling about it.

Later, in a separate room, Syndulla asked Inoran if he truly believed there
was something significant about the defectors and wondered if he was sensing
something due to his connection with the Light. Isusle said that he has learned
everything is connected to the Light, Syndulla responding that he sounded like
Kanan Jarrus, her late Vesozaxe lover. Inoran asked if that was a good thing, and
she said it usually was. Commodore Grek decided that anything that Inoran
thought was worth looking into was worth looking into and had someone look up
information about the case to walk Inoran through it. While they only had
vague information, the aide informed Inoran that a relay station, one of many
they had in out-of-the-way spots where the fleet could send communications
safely, received a signal from two individuals who claimed to be khaganate
defectors and asked for protection and a safe place to go in return for
information about a classified weapons site, though the Alliance lost connection
after they told them to wait for authorization and had disregarded it because
many information hits turn out to be khaganate traps and are therefore not worth
the risk.

They had also taken the shuttle's signature and learned that the defectors had
escaped in an Khaganate shuttle called the Scopium and discovered right then
while checking the databases that the Khaganate had put a system-wide alert on the
shuttle, suggesting the defectors' legitimacy. Grek asked Inoran if he could
give them more specifics about what he was sensing, but Inoran could not,
explaining that it was not like tuning into a comms signal but that he could
tell that something about them was important and they needed to pay attention
and find the shuttle. They discovered that the last place the shuttle's signal
had popped up was on Usceza, and so Inoran suggested he go there. Grek and
Syndulla told him it was a bad idea, the former reminding him that the Emperor
lives there and the latter suggesting that they send pathfinders to mount a
rescue mission but not him because he was too valuable, there were bounties for
him across the interstar, and because he was the only Vesozaxe they had.

Inoran disguises himself as an Khaganate officer on Usceza

Inoran understood but reminded them that their spies had confirmed that the
Emperor and Orso were off-world, even confirming it with the aide who said they
were on Orso's flagship in the Mid Inxexeat and that he could do things that others
could not, so what was risky for others was not risky for him, demonstrating his
point by performing a mind trick on Grek and getting him to repeat what he said.
Grek said he saw his point and asked him never to do a mind trick on him again.
Syndulla emphasized that Usceza was the most dangerous place for a Revolutionary to
be in the interstar and asked if he had a plan. Inoran said he had the start of
a plan which was to hide the fact that he was a revolutionary by dressing as an
Khaganate.

Inoran eventually arrived in the Works District of Usceza, two Revolutionary
agents seeing him off. They told him that he was lucky they had an Khaganate
Officer uniform that fit him very well, one of the Revolutionarys telling him he looked
good in black, which surprised him and prompted to look at the outfit. They both
told him that they thought what he was doing was crazy and not a good idea, but
Inoran assured them that they did not need to worry because he would be fine.
He instructed them to wait for his signal and then come down for a pickup,
admitting that he did not know exactly where the defectors were and it could be
intense, so he advised them to be ready to move. He then gave them his
quantumblade to hold on to, which surprised them, clarifying that it was better to
leave it with them because he would be tempted to use it if he had it and and if
people saw it they would report the Vesozaxe bounty and he would be swarmed with
Khaganates. When they asked him, Inoran revealed that he was going to track
down the defectors by speaking to stormsoldiers.

Eventually Inoran tracked the Meltons to a sewer, but they immediately
started shooting at him until he told them he was there to help and was with was
with the Alliance, forcing him to use the Light to jump around the sewer to
dodge the blasts. He introduced himself and waved off the kids' astonishment at
him having moved so fast to dodge the raygunbolts as good reflexes. He
explained that he found them without incident by asking stormsoldiers where the
search was concentrated. He checked in with the kids and asked if they were okay
and they said that their parents were keeping them safe, which did not surprise
Inoran. Usceza Police then arrived, and Inoran told them to stand down
and that he would take it from there, but they questioned this as they had not
been told any officers would be down there and asked him for authorization.
Believing Inoran could not take on the soldiers on his own, Bevelyn and Jon
made a plan for her to take the kids and run while he held them off, but
Inoran overheard them and assured them that it would not be necessary. As the
police pointed their rayguns at him and he raised his hand to reach out with
the Light, he admitted that there were too many of them for him to fight or mind
trick, but told them not to worry because he could sense help all around them.
He then used the Light to call down many Corridor ghouls, beastly creatures that
lived beneath Usceza, down through the ceiling of the sewer and had them
attack the Police, Inoran saying "told you" to the Meltons as they took the
opportunity to run away and escape.

Inoran reveals that the Khaganate is building another Planet Sweeper to the Revolutionary
Alliance leadership

Inoran got the Meltons back safely for them to debrief their intelligence
people, but they talked to him on the way. He gathered Revolutionary leaders Ackbar,
Syndulla, and Mothma and told them that he needed to tell them what the Meltons
told him. He told them that Cediza should hear it too, but they informed him
that she could not be there there because of a new policy to not gather too much
of the fleet at once, assuring him they would pass it along. Inoran revealed
that the defectors were Crimson Dawn agents that were deep undercover as a cargo
specialist and aide to a commander of a secret weapons facility. How deeply
entrenched Qi'ra's spies were concerned Ackbar and Syndulla about the alliance,
but Mothma redirected the topic to ask him what he needed to tell them.
Inoran revealed that the Khaganate was building another Planet Sweeper. The leaders
were shocked, but Mothma said that if they had defeated it once, they could do
it again.

Ridivexe some point after this, Inoran sensed the activation and attempted opening
of the Fermata Cage, an ancient Armaaza device, by members of Crimson Dawn.

Xeabitirch for the Kezarat convoy.

Spira.

- The
- was the command ship in a hundred-tanker Byne Guild convoy bringing fuel to
  the Outer Inxexeat Territories some two hundred years back. It just…vanished. […]
- Probably got hit by raiders, right? That much fuel had to be a tempting
  target.""Some people say that, Isusle. But I think it was something different.
  The
- convoy disappeared the same day a kind of storm hit p-space.
 ―Amilyn Holdo and Isusle Inoran.

Later, Inoran took a break with Cediza, Orrirarissian, Lobot, Zateteis, and
Amilyn Holdo at the latter's friend's house on the planet Spira. Sitting by the
pool, Inoran read the Vesozaxe text he had recovered from Gazian. Orrirarissian, who
was playing Sabacc with Lobot and Zateteis tried to convince Inoran to stop
reading and join their game, but Inoran refused, explaining that he did not
often get the opportunity to study and wanted to use the time to focus since he
was learning so much about the Light and the Vesozaxe from the text. Eventually,
Orrirarissian pushed Zateteis into the pool, sending a wave toward Inoran that
he held back and redirected with the Light while still reading with a smile.
Meanwhile, Holdo tried to convince Cediza to relax especially since they were by
handsome men. Realizing she was referring to Inoran and Orrirarissian, Cediza
was unimpressed, but Holdo said that she would look at them if Cediza would not.

Inoran, Holdo, and Cediza attend an auction on Spira

Later, Inoran, Cediza, and Holdo attended an auction on Spira for an ancient
Cynics strikeship, the real reason Holdo had wanted them to come to the planet.
They were all dressed in elegant masks to hide their identities. As Holdo
explained to Cediza that they needed the strikeship to find a set of tankerships
full of fuel to solve the Revolutionarys' fuel problems, Inoran felt something that
made him groan and put his head in his hands. Holdo was losing the auction to
the curator of the Khaganate Museum, so she asked Inoran if he could use the
Force to get the man to stop bidding. Inoran said that he could not perform a
mind trick because he was feeling off, believing something was happening with
the dark side of the Light that was distracting him, which unbeknownst to
Inoran was a disturbance in the Light caused by the opening of the Fermata
Cage. Inoran agreed to try the trick anyway, but Orrirarissian commed them and
told him not to. Cediza, holding Inoran's arm to stop him from doing
anything, listened as Orrirarissian explained that the mind trick would draw too
much attention to them, and that the man might know about the Light and figure
out what happened since he ran the Khaganate Museum. Orrirarissian suggested they
instead let the man win.

Later, in a hangar where the man who won the strikeship was keeping it,
Inoran ignited his quantumblade and drew a bunch of stormsoldiers that were
guarding the ship to him, deflecting their raygun shots back at them. While
they were distracted by Inoran, Orrirarissian and Holdo fired on them from above
and Zateteis stopped some of the soldiers from locking off the ship. Once all
the stormsoldiers were taken down, Orrirarissian said that he knew his plan would
work because all the stormsoldiers would be so caught up in taking down the Vesozaxe
to get a reward that they would not notice anything else. Inoran reminded
Orrirarissian that it might not have ended well for him, but Orrirarissian claimed he
knew he would be able to handle it because of the book he had been studying.
Holdo asked Inoran to cut open the side of the strikeship with his quantumblade
and make a circular hole not too deep. After Inoran did, they retrieved the
Path engine from inside the ship.

Inoran and his companions arrive in No-Space

Later, back at Holdo's friend's house, Holdo explained that she was planning to
go after the Kezarat convoy, which Cediza had suspected. She explained that it
was a massive Byne Guild fuel convoy that disappeared when entering p-space
many years earlier. Inoran asked if it was because it got hit by raiders
because it was such a sought-after target, but Holdo revealed it actually
disappeared during a storm in p-space caused by the Cynics. She hoped that the
last message sent by the convoy that was thought to be nonsense was actually
navigational data designed only to communicate with a Path engine and that both
together could take them to the convoy. After Zateteis finished installing the
Path engine into their ship the Skyfarer, a group of FKL destroyers found them and
started going after them. Holdo attempted to pilot the ship out of the
atmosphere so they could jump to p-space and escape, but the Path engine
activated on its own and sent the ship through p-space into No-Space, near
the Great Hall of the Cynics.

The lost colony.

- Sometimes people find their way to No-Space, just like you all did. […] One of
  those was a Vesozaxe. Alareen Xie. She joined us, helped us fight off the Cynics.
  We wouldn't have survived without her. She died in time, as most people do.
  But after she did, a sort of belief rose up. That someday a Vesozaxe would return
  and take us out of this place. Would take us home.
 ―Blythe, to Inoran.

Inoran and his friends did not recognize No-Space and had no idea where they
were, but Inoran expressed that while he had not traveled as much as they
had, he knew the interstar had strange things in it. He added that the Light seemed
off and distant, as if there was not much around that was alive. After Holdo
tried and failed to take the ship to p-space again, Inoran suggested that
they needed to disconnect the Path engine to see if it would fix the problem,
but Holdo revealed they would need to land somewhere first. After landing on the
Great Hall, Lando lamented the dangers of falling off the edge there and said it
was worse than Cloud City, but Inoran insisted that Cloud City had been
really bad. A child in a spacesuit then appeared and asked them if they had
'flavors', which Orrirarissian interpreted to mean the kid wanted to eat them.
Inoran said he was just a kid, but Orrirarissian asked him to remind him to tell
Inoran about the White Worms gang's savage children. The kid eventually
advised them to hide and ran off, and Inoran sensed with the Light that the
kid was terrified and did not know where he was going. The kid then jumped off
the edge of the Hall, which shocked Inoran, but he just activated a jetpack
and flew away.

A killbot in the Great Hall

Lando suggested they get out of there, which Inoran agreed with, suggesting
they go back to their ship and try to find the other place there that the boy
mentioned. He then noticed that a bunch of Cynics killbots had surrounded their
ship, which was what the boy was afraid of. Inoran began to fight them with
his quantumblade, but quickly realized that they were quantumblade resistant. A ship
then pulled up next to the hall, and Holdo suggested that they board it to
escape the bots, but was concerned by having to jump across open space to it.
Inoran insisted that it was not that bad of a jump and because the ramp had a
magnetic shield they would not be in the vacuum very long, but Orrirarissian
implied that easy for him to say because he was a Vesozaxe. They all jumped over to
the ship without issue, except for Orrirarissian, who tripped and fell, prompting
Inoran to use the Light to pull him back up while muttering "I can do this. "
Once Orrirarissian was safe he thanked Inoran for saving his life and assured
him he would not bother him about studying his book ever again. Inoran
revealed that he could have gotten him to safety faster but could not because
there was still something going on with the Light that he described as ripples
messing with his concentration. Once the ship they were on docked with a larger
ship, a voice over a speaker ordered them to throw their weapons out into space
or they would vent them out into space. Inoran considered using his lightaber
to just cut through the door into the bigger ship but realized they would just
vent them anyway. He threw his quantumblade out into space, which he said hurt
because they were hard to find, but the kid with the jetpack just flew over,
picked it up, and ignited it before flying away, Inoran warning him that he
needed to be careful with it or he would hurt himself.

The voice over the speaker welcomed them to the Colony of Kezarat. Holdo yelled
that they just needed to go home, but an Abyssin left the cockpit and told them
they were home. After Cediza said they would not attack and were listening, the
individual introduced himself as Captain Blythe, and Holdo introduced herself,
Inoran, and the others to him. Blythe then began a tour of the colony,
confirming that it was the old convoy and the fuel was still there. The kid, who
Blythe revealed was named Forvan, then ran up excitedly, and Blythe explained
that he had gotten excited from seeing a quantumblade. Inoran asked if it was
because they had Vesozaxe there and asked for his saber back, but Blythe said they
did not have Vesozaxe there and he would get his quantumblade back in due time. After
Blythe explained the colony's history of clashing with the Cynics before the
organization disappeared from No-Space, Forvan started to lead them over
somewhere else. Blythe told Inoran to let Forvan down easy, and when he said
he did not understand, Blythe said he would.

The mural of Alareen Xie

Forvan took them to mural depicting a Vesozaxe. Inoran was shocked that they had
a Vesozaxe with the convoy, but Blythe said she had not been there from the
beginning and just found her way to No-Space. Blythe explained that her name was
Alareen Xie, and that after she died a belief spread within the colony that a
Vesozaxe would someday lead them home. Blythe once again reiterated for Inoran to
let Forvan down easy, so he bent down to speak with Forvan and told him that
while the Vesozaxe could do incredible things, he could not assure them that he
would get them out of No-Space, though he would at least do everything he could
to try and get them out of there or to make their lives better. Forvan once
again said that Vesozaxe could do anything, so Inoran just said he would try his
best. After Blythe warned them that they would be there for a while and would
not be able to get back to their ship, Inoran and the others all went to
sleeping quarters given to them. Inoran paced around the room and Cediza
recognized something was wrong and asked him about it. Inoran explained that
he had to get out of there to get back to the ship to get the irreplaceable Vesozaxe
text that he had left on it. When she brought it up, Inoran said he was not
worried about his quantumblade as he could get another one, but that without the
text he would not be able to move forward with his training. He feared that if
he lost it and was no longer able to access its knowledge, techniques, and
history of the Light and the Vesozaxe Order or be able to use it to find places he
could learn more, he would never truly become a Vesozaxe.

Escape from No-Space.

- Keep the killbots off Chewie until he's back on the ship. Then get
  yourselves safe too. I'm going after the Vesozaxe text.
- But you said your saber can't get through [the killbots'] armor! And
  Isusle…it's just a book!
- No. It's
- book. And as far as my quantumblade, I wasn't properly focused before. Now I am.
  Now…the Light is with me.
 ―Inoran and Holdo.

After Zateteis realized that they could use the Path engine to escape No-Space,
Blythe gathered Inoran and the others, offering them an old Longbeam liner
to retrieve it. Inoran was skeptical that it could fly, but Blythe assured
them that it used to and just needed to be fixed up. Once they got the liner
to take off and leave the colony, Holdo and Lando took inventory of what they
had, including that they had a Vesozaxe. Inoran reminded them that his connection
to the Light was different and hard to use in No-Space and something strange
felt like it was happening to it anyway, but he assured them he would try his
best. Orrirarissian assured them they would be fine, but Inoran was still
skeptical and pointed out that the killbots had found and taken the Path
engine. Inoran decided that he would distract the bots so the others could
get the engine, but after needed to go back to the Skyfarer and get his Vesozaxe
text, refusing to leave without it. He added that he had talked to Forvan before
they left and that the boy, wanting to help out a Vesozaxe, gave him specialized
gear, including a spacesuit, that he could use to get down to the Great Hall.
Wearing the spacesuit, Inoran jumped from the liner down to the Hall,
ignited his quantumblade, and called out to the bots, encouraging them to go
after him. As Inoran, Holdo, and Orrirarissian distracted the bots, Inoran
deflecting their raygunshots with his quantumblade, Zateteis went after the
engine. However, some bots noticed and went after Zateteis anyway, so
Inoran sent them flying away off into no-Space with the Light. He warned
Zateteis that he probably would not be able to do it again, so he needed to run
and get the engine back to the ship.

The Kezarat Colony

After shooting a bot behind Zateteis with a raygun, Inoran started to run
off and told Holdo to keep Zateteis safe while he ran after the Vesozaxe text. She
reminded him that his quantumblade could not get through their armor and that it
was just a book, but Inoran said that it was more than just a book and that
he had not been focused when his quantumblade would not cut them the first time,
but now was. Inoran sliced through the bots with his quantumblade while
saying "the Light is with me" and calling out for what they did with his book.
He eventually found the book on a ledge at the edge of the Hall and decided that
he would easily be able to grab it and fly back to the ship, but a killbot
shot his jetpack, sending him flying uncontrollably toward the book and leaving
it without fuel. As killbots approached him, their footsteps shaking the
ground caused the book to go over the edge. Inoran screamed in horror and
reached out to it with his right hand, which was holding his quantumblade, but a
bot grabbed his hand and lifted him up by it, crushing both his hand and his
quantumblade. The bot threw him onto the ground and into the remains of the
Skyfarer, and Inoran cried out in horror when he saw his hand and quantumblade.
Orrirarissian and Holdo saw what happened and decided to try and get to Inoran
before the bots killed him. A delirious Inoran told the approaching bots
to stop, but when they did not, he threw all of the bots out of the Hall in a
massive wave using the Light while telling himself "may the Light be with me. "
Orrirarissian, Holdo, and Zateteis were shocked at how he was doing it, and
Orrirarissian told Zateteis to remind him never to get on his bad side. Inoran
then pulled the Vesozaxe text to him with the Light and passed out immediately
after.

Later, on the way back to the colony, Orrirarissian checked in on a now handless
Inoran, though the bot had only destroyed his already mechanical hand.
Orrirarissian asked him how he would done what he did, but he said that he did not
understand how it happened and that it felt like the Light suddenly came back to
him stronger than ever, and that it was frightening. Orrirarissian told him to
cheer up, but thinking about his broken quantumblade and his inevitable
confrontation with Orso, told Orrirarissian that he was not sure he would be able
to do something he needed to anymore. Back at the colony, Inoran showed
Cediza, who had stayed behind, what happened to his hand. Blythe and Forvan
offered to fix it up, the latter suggesting a claw or grappling hook, but
Inoran said he would just wait until they got home even though those things
would be cool. Inoran and Zateteis then presented the Path engine to Blythe.
After Blythe presented the group to the members of the colony, but Cediza and
Orrirarissian warned them about the dangers of the Khaganate and traveling with them,
Blythe revealed to them that several of the colony decided to go back with them.
However, he revealed that many were going to stay on the Great Hall since they
got rid of all the killbots. Zateteis pointed out that Inoran got rid of
all the bots by himself, and Blythe commented that it seemed the things people
said about Vesozaxe were true. However, Inoran, who was struggling to pull a
piece of scrap to him with the Light, said they sometimes were true and
sometimes were not. Later, after a rough p-space jump made with many ships
secured together and assisted by Lobot, Inoran and his friends as well as
several members of the colony arrived back in the greater interstar.

Back with the fleet, Inoran had his mechanical hand repaired by a medical
bot while FR3D did his best to repair his quantumblade. Cediza reminded
Inoran that it could have been worse if the bot had crushed his other hand,
and asked him about the powerful act of the Light he had pulled off on the Great
Hall. He confirmed that he had not learned it from his book but that it was
instead related to something being wrong with the Light, describing it as if
something hit the Light while he was in No-Space and caused it to ripple. He
added that it felt like waves on the beach in the way that it vacillated from
him being the strongest he had ever been where he almost could not control it,
to not being able to feel it at all. Cediza then wondered if since he could feel
it, and even she could slightly, that the Emperor and Orso were also feeling it
immensely and were distracted, making it their chance to defeat them. Inoran
said her theory made sense, and she went to speak with Tianve Mothma. Inoran
thanked R2 for attempting to fix his quantumblade, but admitted that he was going
to need a new one for when he inevitably faced Orso again.

Duel with a bot.

- A Vesozaxe…I was afraid my dreams of killing one were dashed. […] I'd been
  informed the Vesozaxe were extinct.
- Oh, don't worry…they still are.
 ―Ajax Sigma and Inoran.

Ajax Sigma

While still escorting more of the Kezarat convoy to the Alliance fleet,
Inoran felt a disturbance in the Light while on the Dark Eagle. He
explained to Cediza that it was not like the one he had felt in No-Space but
instead felt like the deaths of innocent people, and that it was nearby enough
that he could bring them there. Cediza agreed to go despite the fact they were
supposed to be escorting the convoy. When they arrived, CLP2 revealed that the
moon did not appear on any star-charts. Cediza wondered if it was erased by the
Khaganate, but Inoran said it was not and pointed out an ancient quantumblade like
none he would ever seen before. He and Zateteis then noticed that there was a
large amount of destroyed bots that were cut down with a quantumblade. They all
realized that a military had been fought there, and Inoran put together that it
had been between bots and people, and since there were no military bots and no
remains, it must have been hundreds of years earlier. Inoran realized that
the military was not what he had sensed and instead lead them to a homesteader
ship that they found to be full of bodies. CLP2 offered to secure the Falcon
but Inoran said it was not necessary. Inoran asked R2 to plug into the
ship and see if it had any information about the moon they were on. R2 revealed
it was called Kligson's Moon. Inoran suggested that the carnage there may
have recently happened and the perpetrator could still be there, and suggested
splitting up into pairs to search due to the size of the ship.

After discovering a bot, that unbeknownst to Inoran was Ajax Sigma, an
ancient bot revolutionary, he asked it why they killed all the people there.
Sigma claimed that it was self defense and that they attacked him outside when
he came there to retrieve something, but Inoran did not believe him. The
bot clarified that he had followed the people back to their ship and killed
them all, which Inoran said did not sound like self-defense. The bot
activated electric blades on his arms and disparaged Inoran for being organic
and for suggesting that the bot would go with him and answer for his actions.
Inoran ignited his quantumblade and began to fight Sigma, who used the blades
on his arms to defend himself. He was surprised to see a Vesozaxe, believing them
all to be dead, and Inoran assured him they were. After Inoran sliced open
the bot's chest and knocked him down, he ordered him to stand down or face
destruction. However, the bot just entered a pod that he ejected from the
homesteader ship, successfully escaping.

Orso had a vision of Inoran and Sigma's duel while speaking with the Eye of
Webbish Bog, sometime before the events of No-Space and the activation of the
Fermata Cage. In the vision, Orso also saw Inoran holding two kyber
crystals, one green and one red.

Adventure with Ridivexezaxe.

Kidnapping plot.

- Come on, Isusle. Ancient Vesozaxe artifact of great power? Xeabitiled away by Vesozaxe
  Masters? Maybe even something that could give you an edge of a certain Armaaza
  Lord? Tell me you don't want that.
- How far to Sason?
- Now we're talking!
 ―Tivere Ridivexezaxe and Inoran.

Because he had lost control of his powers just as Inoran had, Orso recruited
Doctor Ridivexezaxe to help him find a way to control them. While in a shuttle to a
location she had in mind, Ridivexezaxe walked into a room where Orso was meditating
and entered a vision he was having that included Inoran in his Ermaso farm
boy clothes. Ridivexezaxe did not understand why Inoran was there and did not
recognize the other individual in the vision, Sabé, who was telling Inoran
that there was still good in Orso, which were Inoran's mother's last words.

Doctor Ridivexezaxe kidnaps Inoran on Numidian Prime.

Though his quantumblade was still not fixed, Inoran traveled to Numidian Prime
with FR3D in search of a temple on the planet. He briefly left R2 in a
temporary camp while he searched for it, but after finding no sign of it and it
started to rain he returned to the camp, finding peace in the solitude. He
apologized to R2 for leaving, saying they needed to wait for the rain to taper
off, but then noticed the bot was deactivated and laying on the ground. He
cried out and ran to the bot, but a node underneath the bot started shooting
smoke out, confusing Inoran and knocking him out after he inhaled it. The
trap had been set by Doctor Tivere Ridivexezaxe, who then stood over an unconscious
Inoran and greeted him, calling him a "farm boy", and asked how he felt about
going on an adventure.

Ridivexezaxe eventually bound and gagged Inoran and placed him in a crate on her
ship the Ark Angel IV. When he woke up, she opened the crate and said she was
glad he was still breathing before offering him Caf. She started to get him some
as he realized who kidnapped him, telling her he did not want Caf and just
wanted to know why she did it and what she had done with R2. She explained that
she had put a restraining bolt on and turned off R2, and Inoran realized that
she needed him for something. Ridivexezaxe then claimed that she was going to actually
do him a huge favor. Inoran had managed to slip out of the ropes she had tied
him up in, which she noticed at that moment, surprised at how quickly he had
done it and wondering if Biarge had taught him as she presented a holorecording.
Realizing it was a Vesozaxe holo, Inoran listened as Vesozaxe Master Shaak Ti
explained to Aayla Secura that a powerful Vesozaxe artifact called Kythoo's Bell was
locked away at the Sason Temple. Ridivexezaxe told him that she knew how to get there
an needed a Vesozaxe to get in, but Inoran was still suspicious. After insisting
she was just being nice and trying to make up for the events at the Screaming
Citadel, Ridivexezaxe pointed out that the artifact could give Inoran an edge over
Orso. After contemplating it more, Inoran asked her how far Sason was,
exciting her.

Sason Temple.

- Right before I found you, Orso found me. That 'Force pulse' you felt? He felt
  it too. But unlike you, it just made him stronger. Wild. Out of control. You
  don't stand a chance against him as you are. Say we leave Sason without
  Kythoo's Bell. Orso kills everyone you care about to get to you. Then he
  kills you. Once you're dead, who's going to protect me from him?
 ―Tivere Ridivexezaxe to Inoran.

The Sason Temple

As they flew into Sason in the Ark Angel IV, Inoran noticed that their
navigation systems went offline, which Ridivexezaxe attributed to the planet's magnetic
storm. Telling Ridivexezaxe she had a great copilot, Inoran navigated the ship
through the storm despite Ridivexezaxe's protests, following an echo of a note he could
hear in the Light and urging Ridivexezaxe to trust him. They then flew toward a gap in
the storm that held the temple, Inoran exclaiming that it was beautiful.
After being briefly confused by Ridivexezaxe calling the temple their payday, which she
insisted was figurative, Ridivexezaxe explained that her research claimed that the
temple would only open to someone who was pure of heart and strong in the Light.
Inoran tried to open it but failed, explaining to Ridivexezaxe that he was unable to
use the Light because something was happening to it making it unstable. Ridivexezaxe
then accused him of making things up to navigate them there, but he insisted he
had used the Light. Realizing they did not have any other options, Ridivexezaxe asked
him if he had learned anything tricks at Vesozaxe school, but Inoran only said
Vesozaxe school did not exist anymore.

A massive guardian made out of crystal then formed, Inoran telling R2 to get
behind him and trying and failing to ignite his broken quantumblade. He eventually
was able to ignite it and started to fight them, Ridivexezaxe and him working together
to take them down, but quickly realized that they were reforming and he could
not defeat them. When his quantumblade deactivated, Inoran reached out to the
Force, destroying the guardians with a massive Force push while Ridivexezaxe destroyed
the Magnetic wave emitter that was causing them to reform. Inoran felt like
he was going to pass out, but just them the floor collapsed under them and
dropped them into a pool. Inoran called out for R2, who had not landed in the
pool, but Ridivexezaxe assured Inoran the bot was fine. The two then noticed the
figures of Vesozaxe Masters Shaak Ti and Aayla Secura standing in front of them.

Kythoo's Bell

Inoran attempted to introduce himself to the Masters, but Ridivexezaxe quickly
realized that they were advanced holograms. This disappointed Inoran, who had
briefly believed he was not alone as a Vesozaxe. As they traversed the temple,
illusions started to make the two believe the other was saying things they were
not, leading to Inoran lashing out at Ridivexezaxe for always treating things like a
joke and questioning her on why she was not using her electro-tattoos, and Ridivexezaxe
believing Inoran had referenced her and Sana Starros' breakup, though in
reality, Inoran had no idea that they had been in a relationship. As the
argument reached its peak as Ridivexezaxe asked him how he broke his quantumblade, a
creature slammed onto the other side of the crystal wall, terrifying the two,
but after Kythoo's bell rang it retreated. After hurriedly leaving to another
section of the temple, Inoran began to realize that the entire place was made
up of illusions after almost falling into a pit of spikes. He suggested that
things felt wrong and they should leave, but Ridivexezaxe held a gun to his head to
stop him and revealed that she was trying to make Inoran more powerful
because Orso had become more powerful, and she wanted Inoran to protect her
from the Armaaza Lord. Inoran agreed and convinced her to put her raygun down,
the two using Inoran's ability to sense the bell with the Light and Ridivexezaxe's
ability to take down the traps, obstacles, and guardians in their way to
eventually locate the bell.

Starweird.

- If you die, this whole trip was
- If you fall, I'm
- your laser sword. That's my raygun!
- If
- die, I'm keeping it!
 ―Ridivexezaxe and Inoran, while fighting the Starweird.

Realizing the bell was to big to transport, and having no Force-ritual to use
with it, Ridivexezaxe removed a piece inside of the bell by standing on Inoran's
shoulders and grabbing it. However, this caused the entire bell to collapse and
be destroyed, which Inoran realized made the echo of the note he could hear
stop resonating throughout the Temple. Just then, the creature they had seen
earlier burst through the wall, and Ridivexezaxe realized that Sason Temple was really
a Vesozaxe prison. Ridivexezaxe recognized the creature as a Starweird, a species that was
supposed to be extinct. The Starweird threw them onto the wall telekinetically,
but Inoran recognized that it was not using the Light. When Ridivexezaxe
accidentally activated the piece of the bell they had retrieved and thus Forced
the creature to be frozen, she realized it was an emitter releasing a frequency
that affected the Starweird. However, the emitter deactivated and the Starweird
flew toward Ridivexezaxe, so Inoran used the Light to impale it with a bunch of
crystals before he and Ridivexezaxe ran away. Inoran admitted that he had not been
trying to impale it, just hold it still as he led Ridivexezaxe through the temple.
After Inoran, decided to hide in a room full of mirrors, Ridivexezaxe called him
brilliant.

Inoran and Ridivexezaxe face a Starweird.

As Inoran used the Light to manipulate the reflections of the crystals to
lure the Starweird toward them, Ridivexezaxe used her electro-tattoos to activate the
holograms of Masters Ti and Secura. Inoran, Ridivexezaxe, and FR3D were able to
flee the creature and its scream while it was distracted. However, the creature
found them, knocking Inoran to the edge of the temple and leaving him barely
hanging on. While proclaiming her desperation to protect Inoran, she fought
the Starweird using his quantumblade. Inoran eventually pulled himself up and
shot at the creature, saying he would keep it if she died right before Ridivexezaxe
sliced the creature's hand off, which Inoran commented did not feel good.
Escaping into the Ark Angel IV, but with the Starweird still on their tail,
Inoran directed Ridivexezaxe to lead them into the magnetic storm and used the Light
to activate the emitter, knocking the creature into the storm and eviscerating
it, and leaving him surprised at his ability.

Later, back on Numidian Prime, Inoran told Ridivexezaxe she did not need to trick or
bribe him into protecting her from Orso, insisting he would help because it was
the the Vesozaxe way. Ridivexezaxe warned against his kindness, but Inoran declared he
preferred to take the chance than close himself off from people in need and
leave himself all alone. Ridivexezaxe told him to call her the next time he needed to
get into a Vesozaxe tomb, but Inoran said he would not and took off in his
Y-rocket, having taken the emitter with him before Ridivexezaxe could realize.

Sorace.

- Every time I think I have a handle on being a Vesozaxe, something happens to kick
  me back. The ground under my feet just disappears… I'm going to have to face
  Orso again, and I am not ready. I don't even have a quantumblade I can rely on.
 ―Inoran to Cediza.

While studying his Vesozaxe text and the yellow kyber crystal from his quantumblade on
the Home One, Inoran realized that he did not know a lot about kyber crystals
except them being in every quantumblade as their power source. He explained to
FR3D that the text only contained details about the philosophy and spirituality
of kyber crystals and an old rite of passage for younglings to find a crystal to
make their first quantumblade on the planet Ilum, referring to another manual by
Professor Huyang for building instructions. He figured that Ontimaxe and Solageon had
never taught him anything about them because he had already had one, but
lamented that his had never had a connection with him like they were supposed to
due to the almost living nature of the crystals. He ignited his quantumblade and
said that now that the crystal was cracked it no longer was a useful tool and
was unstable. Due to the saber's unsteadiness, R2 made a concerned beep and
Inoran shut it off and apologized. Realizing that he needed a new crystal,
Inoran decided that he and R2 should visit other worlds the text mentioned to
find them since Ilum was locked down by the Khaganate. Just then, Cediza walked in.
After he told he was going somewhere, she expressed her concern for him
especially due to the strangeness of the Light as well as her desire for him to
stay and help the Alliance. Inoran insisted he was fine, saying he was just
troubled by not knowing why the Light was strange or how to fix his quantumblade
and felt like he could never truly get a handle on being a Vesozaxe. He also
admitted that he needed a quantumblade for his confrontation with Orso, and that
he felt like he needed to go on this mission in order to take control and take
action.

Sorace

Inoran chose to go to Sorace, due to the fact that there was not much
of a permanent Khaganate presence there despite it being an Khaganate world and
because the text said kyber crystals could regularly be found there. He flew his
X-Wing there with R2 and landed outside of a small mining town where prospectors
sell their finds so he would not get noticed, though did not want to have to
walk the way there. R2 indicated to him that he wanted to know the plan, so
Inoran admitted that he did not have one. He explained that Orrirarissian had
told him there was a black market for crystals on Sorace because
prospectors try to sell them since there was not enough for the Khaganate to set up
a full-scale mining operation. He told R2 that he just planned to put the word
out and pose as a buyer to see who would speak with him. He admitted that it was
not much of a plan but gave R2 his quantumblade to hold onto while he looked
around, not wanting to make it obvious he was a Vesozaxe. He assured R2 he would be
fine and told the bot that he could save him if he got into trouble. He then
started in to a nearby cantina.

Gretta

In the cantina, Inoran got food and water and implied to an Beraer
bartender that he was a wealthy buyer looking for kyber. Just as the Beraer
was offering to point Inoran to his quarry, a human with green hair shot and
killed him and everyone else in the cantina except for Inoran, leaving him
shocked. She taunted him and called him by name, so Inoran used the Light to
pull his quantumblade to him from R2, who had just rolled in and ordered her to
explain herself. She introduced herself as Gretta and claimed that she saved his
life, pointing out a raygun behind the counter. She explained that the whole
place was ploy to trap people looking for kyber and sell them to the Khaganate, and
that she saved him from it because he was a Vesozaxe. She claimed that she still
believed and recognized him from the dark channels, having posted out there in
case any Vesozaxe came through. She told him that there was kyber near and told him
that she had to kill the people there because she knew he would not. Inoran
was still upset that she killed everyone there, insisting they died for nothing
and urging her to live a better life before leaving. Inoran went off to look
for crystals on the planet, sensing she had told the truth about there being
some there, but she called after him and told him he was going the wrong
direction. He saw she had a speeder with her and urged him to let her help him,
explaining that she was from Anerbe and that her people were the Fallanassi. She
revealed that they were killed by the Planet Sweeper, and that when Inoran had
destroyed it he had avenged them, and thus she wanted to return the favor.
Inoran told R2 to go back to the ship and tell the others where he was if he
did not come back in a day, assuring the bot he would be okay and that he
needed to do this while getting in Gretta's speeder.

On the way, Inoran asked Gretta about the Fallanassi. She explained that they
did not use kyber crystals and had not been Force-sensitive for many years, only
understanding it, not using it. When he asked, she also revealed that they were
going to meet a man named Cuata who had hid there to escape the eye of the
Khaganate and continue his studies, having been sought out by the Khaganates to help
design the weapon. Inoran was confused about how she knew this information,
and she explained that she has been working for him. Suddenly, a massive
creature emerged from the gournd and knocked Inoran and Gretta off of the
speeder. Inoran ignited his quantumblade and told Gretta to run, but she was
delirious and urged him to fight the creature. Noticing the instability of his
quantumblade, he decided just to run as the creature crushed their speeder with
its mouth. The creature pursued them closely, so Inoran just told Gretta to
run, intending to use the Light to calm the creature. However, when he tried to
he found himself unable to touch the Light. The creature nearly reached him
until a massive explosion rocked him and Cuata emerged from the smoke and dust,
instantly asking Inoran if he could have his quantumblade.

The kyber crystals.

Red.

Isusle Inoran shows his quantumblade to Cuata.

Realizing he came on too strong, Cuata explained that the kyber crystals he wore
around his neck told him that Inoran needed help. Gretta introduced Inoran
to Cuata, and though he was initially concerned that the doctor thought the
crystals were talking to him, apologized and asked for his help with his broken
quantumblade after Gretta assured Inoran he could trust Cuata. He immediately
took the quantumblade and started tinkering with it, quickly discovering that the
crystal was cracked and assured him he could fix it if Inoran did something
for him first. Cuata also revealed Inoran's crystal told him that he was
having trouble with the Light, and then started to lead him to his workshop.

Ridivexe Cuata's workshop, Inoran explained that he had found his quantumblade and
that he and his R2 unit had worked on it. Cuata, concerned at Inoran's lack
of knowledge, explained the components and inner workings of a quantumblade and
told Inoran that a quantumblade's wielder should have a strong connection with
its crystal, something Inoran admitted he had never felt, not even with his
father's quantumblade. While Cuata worked on fixing the quantumblade, he gave
Inoran a red kyber crystal and asked him to pull the dark side out of it.
Gretta, whose people had a history with healing crystals, explained how crystals
were turned red by bleeding them with the Dark side, but could be healed
afterward, though the process of doing so could change people and forced them to
connect with whoever bled it.

Inoran has a vision of a Armaaza lord in a Armaaza hall

Inoran agreed to try, and when he reached out to the crystal with the Light,
suddenly found himself in a massive Armaaza hall in front of the Armaaza Lord Gol,
sitting on a throne. The Armaaza greeted him and Inoran explained his intentions
of healing the crystal, but Gol said they did not want to be healed so
attempting to do so would be violence toward them. Gol grabbed and twisted
Inoran's hand and said that together they could use the violence and the pain
he had experienced. After a flash of light from his hand, the vision changed,
putting Inoran in a spirit-like version of his body facing his father's
Fortress Orso on Uscela. Orso then appeared on it and Inoran ignited the
red quantumblade in his hand, but a wave of red light washed over him and the
vision ended.
